class="hw-btn" style="background:#f59e0b;color:#fff" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">📘 Learn</button><div class="hw-panel"><div class="ex-card"><h4>Examples & Practice 💬</h4><div class="ex-item">1) Doing household <span class="vt-word">chores<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>chore</b><br>домашняя обязанность, дело</span></span> every weekend keeps the house organized and prevents mess from accumulating in every room. 🏠</div><div class="ex-item">2) I need to separate the <span class="vt-word">laundry<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>laundry</b><br>стирка, грязное бельё</span></span> into whites and colors to avoid dye transfer and keep clothes looking bright and new. 👕</div><div class="ex-item">3) Use the right <span class="vt-word">detergent<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>detergent</b><br>моющее средство, порошок</span></span> for delicate fabrics, or they might shrink or lose their soft texture during the wash cycle. 🧴</div><div class="ex-item">4) Please run the <span class="vt-word">vacuum<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>vacuum</b><br>пылесос</span></span> over the living room carpet before guests arrive, because pet hair and crumbs are visible on the floor. 🌪️</div><div class="ex-item">5) After sweeping, use a damp <span class="vt-word">mop<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>mop</b><br>швабра</span></span> <span class="prep">to</span> clean the kitchen tiles thoroughly and leave them shining without streaks. 💧</div><div class="ex-item">6) Keep a sturdy <span class="vt-word">broom<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>broom</b><br>метла, веник</span></span> near the back door so you can quickly sweep away dirt and leaves before they get tracked inside. 🧹</div><div class="ex-item">7) Store dry goods in airtight containers inside the <span class="vt-word">cupboard<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>cupboard</b><br>шкаф, кухонный шкафчик</span></span> to protect them from moisture, pests, and stale air. 📦</div><div class="ex-item">8) Treat that red wine <span class="vt-word">stain<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>stain</b><br>пятно, загрязнение</span></span> immediately with cold water and salt, or it will permanently damage the tablecloth. 🍷</div><div class="ex-item">9) This kitchen <span class="vt-word">appliance<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>appliance</b><br>бытовой прибор</span></span> has a self-cleaning function, but you still need to wipe the exterior regularly to remove grease and fingerprints. 🍳</div><div class="ex-item">10) Throw the empty bottle in the recycling <span class="vt-word">bin<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>bin</b><br>мусорное ведро</span></span> to help reduce landfill waste and support sustainable habits at home. ♻️</div></div><div class="practice-box"><h4>🎯 Practice Text: Weekend Cleaning 🗓️</h4><div class="practice-text">Every Saturday, I clean my house. First, I do my <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">chores 🏠</span><span class="tooltip">📌 обязанности</span></span>: laundry, vacuuming, and dusting. I sort the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">laundry 👕</span><span class="tooltip">📌 бельё</span></span> by color and add the right <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">detergent 🧴</span><span class="tooltip">📌 порошок</span></span>. While the machine works, I sweep the floors with my <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">broom 🧹</span><span class="tooltip">📌 метла</span></span>. Then I use the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">mop 💧</span><span class="tooltip">📌 швабра</span></span> for the kitchen and bathroom. For carpets, I use the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">vacuum 🌪️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 пылесос</span></span> to remove dust and pet hair. If I see a <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">stain 🍷</span><span class="tooltip">📌 пятно</span></span>, I clean it quickly. After that, I wipe the kitchen <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">appliance 🍳</span><span class="tooltip">📌 прибор</span></span>. Finally, I empty the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">bin ♻️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 ведро</span></span> and organize the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">cupboard 📦</span><span class="tooltip">📌 шкафчик</span></span>. A clean home starts with simple steps!</div></div></div><button class="hw-btn test" style="background:#ef4444;color:#fff" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">🧪 Test</button><div class="hw-panel"><h3 class="qz-title">🧪 Test: Nouns</h3><p class="qz-sub">Choose the correct option:</p><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">1</div><div class="qz-sent">Doing household ___ every weekend keeps the house organized.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q1" id="hw_n_q1a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_n_q1a" class="qz-opt">A) chores</label><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q1" id="hw_n_q1b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_n_q1b" class="qz-opt">B) laundry</label><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q1" id="hw_n_q1c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_n_q1c" class="qz-opt">C) detergent</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Chores</b> = домашние обязанности.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ laundry = стирка. detergent = моющее средство.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">2</div><div class="qz-sent">Separate the ___ into whites and colors before washing.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q2" id="hw_n_q2a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_n_q2a" class="qz-opt">A) chores</label><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q2" id="hw_n_q2b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_n_q2b" class="qz-opt">B) laundry</label><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q2" id="hw_n_q2c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_n_q2c" class="qz-opt">C) vacuum</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Laundry</b> = бельё, стирка.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ chores = обязанности. vacuum = пылесос.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">3</div><div class="qz-sent">Use the right ___ for delicate fabrics to avoid damage.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q3" id="hw_n_q3a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_n_q3a" class="qz-opt">A) mop</label><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q3" id="hw_n_q3b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_n_q3b" class="qz-opt">B) detergent</label><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q3" id="hw_n_q3c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_n_q3c" class="qz-opt">C) broom</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Detergent</b> = моющее средство.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ mop = швабра. broom = метла.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">4</div><div class="qz-sent">Run the ___ over the carpet to remove pet hair.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q4" id="hw_n_q4a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_n_q4a" class="qz-opt">A) vacuum</label><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q4" id="hw_n_q4b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_n_q4b" class="qz-opt">B) stain</label><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q4" id="hw_n_q4c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_n_q4c" class="qz-opt">C) appliance</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Vacuum</b> = пылесос.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ stain = пятно. appliance = прибор.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">5</div><div class="qz-sent">Use a damp ___ to clean kitchen tiles thoroughly.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q5" id="hw_n_q5a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_n_q5a" class="qz-opt">A) broom</label><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q5" id="hw_n_q5b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_n_q5b" class="qz-opt">B) mop</label><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q5" id="hw_n_q5c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_n_q5c" class="qz-opt">C) bin</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Mop</b> = швабра.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ broom = метла. bin = ведро.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">6</div><div class="qz-sent">Keep a sturdy ___ near the door to sweep away leaves.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q6" id="hw_n_q6a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_n_q6a" class="qz-opt">A) broom</label><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q6" id="hw_n_q6b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_n_q6b" class="qz-opt">B) cupboard</label><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q6" id="hw_n_q6c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_n_q6c" class="qz-opt">C) stain</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Broom</b> = метла, веник.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ cupboard = шкаф. stain = пятно.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">7</div><div class="qz-sent">Store dry goods inside the ___ to protect from moisture.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q7" id="hw_n_q7a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_n_q7a" class="qz-opt">A) appliance</label><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q7" id="hw_n_q7b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_n_q7b" class="qz-opt">B) cupboard</label><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q7" id="hw_n_q7c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_n_q7c" class="qz-opt">C) bin</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Cupboard</b> = кухонный шкаф.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ appliance = прибор. bin = мусорное ведро.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">8</div><div class="qz-sent">Treat that red wine ___ immediately with cold water.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q8" id="hw_n_q8a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_n_q8a" class="qz-opt">A) stain</label><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q8" id="hw_n_q8b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_n_q8b" class="qz-opt">B) appliance</label><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q8" id="hw_n_q8c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_n_q8c" class="qz-opt">C) chores</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Stain</b> = пятно.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ appliance = прибор. chores = обязанности.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">9</div><div class="qz-sent">This kitchen ___ has a self-cleaning function.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q9" id="hw_n_q9a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_n_q9a" class="qz-opt">A) appliance</label><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q9" id="hw_n_q9b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_n_q9b" class="qz-opt">B) bin</label><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q9" id="hw_n_q9c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_n_q9c" class="qz-opt">C) mop</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! 💬</h4><div class="ex-item">1) Use a stiff brush to <span class="vt-word">scrub<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>scrub</b><br>тереть, чистить щёткой</span></span> the bathroom tiles <span class="prep">with</span> cleaning solution to remove built-up soap scum and mold. 🧽</div><div class="ex-item">2) After cleaning, gently <span class="vt-word">polish<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>polish</b><br>полировать, натирать</span></span> the wooden table <span class="prep">with</span> a soft cloth to restore its shine and protect the surface from scratches. ✨</div><div class="ex-item">3) Remember to <span class="vt-word">wipe<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>wipe</b><br>вытирать</span></span> the kitchen counters <span class="prep">with</span> an antibacterial spray after cooking to keep the area hygienic and ready for the next meal. 🍽️</div><div class="ex-item">4) Lightly <span class="vt-word">dust<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>dust</b><br>вытирать пыль</span></span> the bookshelves and picture frames <span class="prep">with</span> a microfiber cloth, as dust can trigger allergies if left for too long. 🌬️</div><div class="ex-item">5) <span class="vt-word">Sweep<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>sweep</b><br>подметать</span></span> the porch before it rains, so wet leaves won't stick to the concrete and become difficult to remove later. 🍂</div><div class="ex-item">6) Carefully <span class="vt-word">fold<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>fold</b><br>складывать</span></span> each shirt <span class="prep">into</span> neat rectangles before placing them in the drawer to save space and prevent wrinkles. 👔</div><div class="ex-item">7) You should <span class="vt-word">iron<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>iron</b><br>гладить</span></span> your uniform the night before so you're not rushed in the morning and look professional for work. 🧵</div><div class="ex-item">8) Let's <span class="vt-word">tidy<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>tidy</b><br>прибирать, наводить порядок</span></span> the living room <span class="prep">up</span> quickly — the guests will arrive in thirty minutes and we need everything looking presentable. 🛋️</div><div class="ex-item">9) After the clothes are dry, <span class="vt-word">load<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>load</b><br>загружать, загружать в машину</span></span> them <span class="prep">into</span> the dryer immediately to avoid mildew smell and keep fabrics fresh. 🔄</div><div class="ex-item">10) <span class="vt-word">Unload<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>unload</b><br>выгружать</span></span> the dishwasher carefully <span class="prep">before</span> dinner starts so you can set the table without waiting for clean plates. 🍴</div></div><div class="practice-box"><h4>🎯 Practice Text: Cleaning Day 🧹</h4><div class="practice-text">I start my cleaning day by deciding what to <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">tidy 🛋️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 прибирать</span></span> first. I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">sweep 🍂</span><span class="tooltip">📌 подметать</span></span> the floors, then <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">dust 🌬️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 вытирать пыль</span></span> all surfaces. If there is dirt, I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">scrub 🧽</span><span class="tooltip">📌 тереть</span></span> it carefully. I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">wipe 🍽️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 вытирать</span></span> the counters and <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">polish ✨</span><span class="tooltip">📌 полировать</span></span> the furniture. After washing clothes, I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">load 🔄</span><span class="tooltip">📌 загружать</span></span> the dryer and later <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">unload 🍴</span><span class="tooltip">📌 выгружать</span></span> it. I always <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">fold 👔</span><span class="tooltip">📌 складывать</span></span> and <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">iron 🧵</span><span class="tooltip">📌 гладить</span></span> carefully. class="prep">into</span> separate bins to make the process easier for the waste collection team. 🗑️</div><div class="ex-item">8) After the laundry is dry, <span class="vt-word">hang up<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>hang up</b><br>развешивать</span></span> the shirts <span class="prep">on</span> wooden hangers to preserve their shape and avoid ironing later. 👗</div><div class="ex-item">9) You made a mistake painting the wall, so you'll have to <span class="vt-word">do it over<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>do over</b><br>переделывать</span></span> from scratch to match the rest perfectly. 🎨</div><div class="ex-item">10) Remember to <span class="vt-word">take out<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>take out</b><br>выносить</span></span> the rubbish <span class="prep">before</span> collection day, or the bin will overflow and smell terrible. 🚛</div><div class="ex-item">11) Can you <span class="vt-word">put it away<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>put away</b><br>убирать на место</span></span> properly? The toys are scattered all over the floor again. 🧸</div><div class="ex-item">12) I need to <span class="vt-word">throw them out<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>throw out</b><br>выбрасывать</span></span> — these old magazines are just collecting dust on the shelves. 📰</div><div class="ex-item">13) She always <span class="vt-word">cleans it up<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>clean up</b><br>убирать</span></span> immediately after cooking, never leaving dirty dishes in the sink overnight. 🍽️</div><div class="ex-item">14) Please <span class="vt-word">tidy it up<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>tidy up</b><br>прибирать</span></span> before the guests arrive — the living room looks like a tornado hit it! 🌪️</div><div class="ex-item">15) We should <span class="vt-word">sort them out<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>sort out</b><br>разбирать</span></span> by color and fabric type before putting them in the washing machine. 👕</div></div><div class="practice-box"><h4>🎯 Practice Text: Household Flow 🔄</h4><div class="practice-text">Every evening, I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">clean up 🧼</span><span class="tooltip">📌 убирать</span></span> the kitchen and <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">wipe down 🦠</span><span class="tooltip">📌 протирать</span></span> all counters thoroughly. I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">clear away 🍲</span><span class="tooltip">📌 убирать со стола</span></span> dishes and help <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">sort out 🗑️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 сортировать</span></span> recycling into different bins. I always <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">put away 🧸</span><span class="tooltip">📌 убирать на место</span></span> items in their proper spots — if I leave things out, they create clutter. When the floors are dirty, I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">tidy up 🛋️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 прибирать</span></span> the whole room systematically. On wash day, I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">hang up 👗</span><span class="tooltip">📌 развешивать</span></span> clothes to dry immediately. I never <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">throw out ♻️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 выбрасывать</span></span> useful items without checking if they can be donated or recycled. When I make a mistake while cleaning or organizing, I just <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">do it over 🎨</span><span class="tooltip">📌 переделывать</span></span> carefully. And of course, I always <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">take out 🚛</span><span class="tooltip">📌 выносить</span></span> the trash before it overflows. If something is dirty, I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">clean it up 🧽</span><span class="tooltip">📌 чистить</span></span> right away. If toys are scattered, I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">put them away 🧸</span><span class="tooltip">📌 убирать их</span></span> immediately. If surfaces are dusty, I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">wipe them down 🧹</span><span class="tooltip">📌 протирать их</span></span>. If clothes are wrinkled, I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">tidy them up 👔</span><span class="tooltip">📌 приводить в порядок</span></span>. Good habits make life easier!</div></div></div><button class="hw-btn test" style="background:#2563eb;color:#fff" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">🧪 Test</button><div class="hw-panel"><h3 class="qz-title">🧪 Test: Phrasal Verbs</h3><p class="qz-sub">Choose the correct option:</p><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">1</div><div class="qz-sent">Let's ___ the kitchen after cooking so it stays hygienic.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_pv_q1" id="hw_pv_q1a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_pv_q1a" class="qz-opt">A) clean up</label><input type="radio" name="hw_pv_q1" id="hw_pv_q1b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_pv_q1b" class="qz-opt">B) throw out</label><input type="radio" name="hw_pv_q1" id="hw_pv_q1c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_pv_q1c" class="qz-opt">C) hang up</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! best. 🧩</div><div class="ex-item">10) I prefer to <span class="vt-word">do the ironing<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>do the ironing</b><br>гладить</span></span> in the evening while watching TV, because Every morning, I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">make the bed 🛏️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 заправлять кровать</span></span>. After breakfast, my family helps <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">clear the table 🍽️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 убирать со стола</span></span>. I usually <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">do the laundry 👕</span><span class="tooltip">📌 стирать</span></span> on Saturdays, while others <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">take out the trash 🗑️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 выносить мусор</span></span>. When cooking, we all <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">set the table ️</span><span class="tooltip"> накрывать на стол</span></span> nicely. If it gets messy, we <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">put things in order </span><span class="tooltip"> привести в порядок</span></span> quickly. We try to <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">keep things tidy ✨</span><span class="tooltip">📌 поддерживать порядок</span></span> daily. If someone needs help, we gladly <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">lend a hand 🤝</span><span class="tooltip">📌 помочь</span></span>. Everyone <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">pitches in 🧩</span><span class="tooltip">📌 подключиться</span></span>, and in the evening, I finally <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">do the ironing </span><span class="tooltip">📌 гладить</span></span>. class="hw-btn" style="background:#eab308;color:#fff" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">📘 Learn</button><div class="hw-panel"><div class="ex-card"><h4>Examples & Practice 💬</h4><div class="ex-item">1) Store your tools <span class="vt-word">tidily<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>tidily</b><br>аккуратно, опрятно</span></span> in labeled boxes so you can find exactly what you need without rummaging through messy drawers. 📦</div><div class="ex-item">2) The stain wasn't removed <span class="vt-word">adequately<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>adequately</b><br>достаточно, надлежащим образом</span></span> because we used the wrong type of detergent for that specific fabric. 🧴</div><div class="ex-item">3) If you assemble the shelf <span class="vt-word">improperly<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>improperly</b><br>неправильно, ненадлежащим образом</span></span>, it might collapse under weight and damage your floor or walls. 🛠️</div><div class="ex-item">4) The guests behaved <span class="vt-word">disrespectfully<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>disrespectfully</b><br>неуважительно</span></span> by leaving muddy shoes on the clean hallway rug without wiping them first. 👞</div><div class="ex-item">5) It's <span class="vt-word">unfair<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>unfairly</b><br>несправедливо</span></span> to expect one person to handle all the cleaning after a large party when everyone helped cook the food. ⚖️</div><div class="ex-item">6) You spelled the cleaning product names <span class="vt-word">incorrectly<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>incorrectly</b><br>неправильно</span></span> on the shopping list, so I accidentally bought floor polish instead of glass cleaner. 📝</div><div class="ex-item">7) Complaining <span class="vt-word">unreasonably<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>unreasonably</b><br>необоснованно</span></span> about minor dust won't solve anything — it's better to just grab a cloth and wipe it down quickly. 🌪️</div><div class="ex-item">8) He <span class="vt-word">misguidedly<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>misguidedly</b><br>ошибочно, по неверному совету</span></span> mixed two different cleaning chemicals, creating a dangerous gas that required opening all windows immediately. ☣️</div><div class="ex-item">9) The plumber <span class="vt-word">unexpectedly<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>unexpectedly</b><br>неожиданно</span></span> arrived early for the repair, which was actually convenient because we were home all day. 🔧</div><div class="ex-item">10) The heavy sofa was placed <span class="vt-word">inconveniently<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>inconveniently</b><br>неудобно</span></span> in front of the window, blocking natural light and making the room feel cramped and dark. 🛋️</div></div><div class="practice-box"><h4>🎯 Practice Text: Mindful Habits ⚡</h4><div class="practice-text">To maintain a good home, organize things <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">tidily 📦</span><span class="tooltip">📌 аккуратно</span></span>. Don't let mess grow because it won't resolve <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">adequately 🧴</span><span class="tooltip">📌 должным образом</span></span> on its own. If assembled <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">improperly 🛠️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 неправильно</span></span>, things break. Never act <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">disrespectfully 👞</span><span class="tooltip">📌 неуважительно</span></span> toward shared spaces. It's <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">unfairly ⚖️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 несправедливо</span></span> to dump work on one person. Label items <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">incorrectly 📝</span><span class="tooltip">📌 неправильно</span></span> and you waste time. Don't complain <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">unreasonably 🌪️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 необоснованно</span></span>. <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">Misguidedly ☣️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 ошибочно</span></span> mixing chemicals is dangerous. If help arrives <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">unexpectedly 🔧</span><span class="tooltip">📌 неожиданно</span></span>, welcome it. Place furniture <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">inconveniently 🛋️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 неудобно</span></span> and you'll regret it. Small mindful choices make big differences!</div></div></div><button class="hw-btn test" style="background:#ca8a04;color:#fff" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">🧪 Test</button><div class="hw-panel"><h3 class="qz-title">🧪 Test: Adverbs</h3><p class="qz-sub">Choose the correct option:</p><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">1</div><div class="qz-sent">She arrived ___ for the interview and made a great first impression.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q1" id="hw_adv_q1a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adv_q1a" class="qz-opt">A) punctually</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q1" id="hw_adv_q1b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q1b" class="qz-opt">B) unpunctually</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q1" id="hw_adv_q1c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q1c" class="qz-opt">C) inconsistently</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Punctually</b> = пунктуально, вовремя.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ unpunctually = непунктуально. inconsistently = непоследовательно.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">2</div><div class="qz-sent">The team worked ___ and finished the project two days early.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q2" id="hw_adv_q2a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q2a" class="qz-opt">A) inefficiently</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q2" id="hw_adv_q2b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adv_q2b" class="qz-opt">B) efficiently</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q2" id="hw_adv_q2c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q2c" class="qz-opt">C) unnecessarily</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Efficiently</b> = эффективно.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ inefficiently = неэффективно. unnecessarily = без необходимости.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">3</div><div class="qz-sent">He exercises ___, sometimes daily, sometimes not at all.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q3" id="hw_adv_q3a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q3a" class="qz-opt">A) regularly</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q3" id="hw_adv_q3b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adv_q3b" class="qz-opt">B) irregularly</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q3" id="hw_adv_q3c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q3c" class="qz-opt">C) patiently</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Irregularly</b> = нерегулярно.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ regularly = регулярно. patiently = терпеливо.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">4</div><div class="qz-sent">She submits her work ___, which makes planning difficult.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q4" id="hw_adv_q4a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adv_q4a" class="qz-opt">A) inconsistently</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q4" id="hw_adv_q4b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q4b" class="qz-opt">B) consistently</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q4" id="hw_adv_q4c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q4c" class="qz-opt">C) punctually</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Inconsistently</b> = непоследовательно, нестабильно.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ consistently = последовательно. punctually = пунктуально.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">5</div><div class="qz-sent">You're ___ complicating a simple task; just follow the steps.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q5" id="hw_adv_q5a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q5a" class="qz-opt">A) necessarily</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q5" id="hw_adv_q5b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adv_q5b" class="qz-opt">B) unnecessarily</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q5" id="hw_adv_q5c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q5c" class="qz-opt">C) appropriately</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Unnecessarily</b> = без необходимости, излишне.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ necessarily = обязательно. appropriately = уместно.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">6</div><div class="qz-sent">The child waited ___ for his turn, without complaining.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q6" id="hw_adv_q6a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adv_q6a" class="qz-opt">A) patiently</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q6" id="hw_adv_q6b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q6b" class="qz-opt">B) impatiently</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q6" id="hw_adv_q6c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q6c" class="qz-opt">C) inadvertently</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Patiently</b> = терпеливо.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ impatiently = нетерпеливо. inadvertently = нечаянно.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">7</div><div class="qz-sent">He ___ deleted the important file; it was a mistake.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q7" id="hw_adv_q7a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q7a" class="qz-opt">A) appropriately</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q7" id="hw_adv_q7b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adv_q7b" class="qz-opt">B) inadvertently</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q7" id="hw_adv_q7c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q7c" class="qz-opt">C) responsibly</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Inadvertently</b> = нечаянно, по неосторожности.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ appropriately = уместно. responsibly = ответственно.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">8</div><div class="qz-sent">___, the train was cancelled, so we missed the meeting.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q8" id="hw_adv_q8a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q8a" class="qz-opt">A) fortunately</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q8" id="hw_adv_q8b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adv_q8b" class="qz-opt">B) unfortunately</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q8" id="hw_adv_q8c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q8c" class="qz-opt">C) necessarily</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Unfortunately</b> = к сожалению.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ fortunately = к счастью. necessarily = обязательно.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">9</div><div class="qz-sent">It's ___ to wear shorts to a formal business meeting.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q9" id="hw_adv_q9a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q9a" class="qz-opt">A) appropriately</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q9" id="hw_adv_q9b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adv_q9b" class="qz-opt">B) inappropriately</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q9" id="hw_adv_q9c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q9c" class="qz-opt">C) punctually</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Inappropriately</b> = неуместно.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ appropriately = уместно. punctually = пунктуально.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">10</div><div class="qz-sent">Leaving the door unlocked was ___ and put everyone at risk.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q10" id="hw_adv_q10a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q10a" class="qz-opt">A) responsibly</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q10" id="hw_adv_q10b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adv_q10b" class="qz-opt">B) irresponsibly</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q10" id="hw_adv_q10c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q10c" class="qz-opt">C) efficiently</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Irresponsibly</b> = безответственно.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ responsibly = ответственно. efficiently = эффективно.</div></div></div></div></div></div>

translateY(5px)}.blur-wrapper:hover .tooltip{transform:translateX(0) translateY(0)}}</style><div class="hw-wrap"><div class="hw-box"><h2 class="hw-title">🧹 Housework & Cleaning: 🏷️ Adjectives</h2><button class="hw-btn" style="background:#06b6d4;color:#fff" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">📘 Learn</button><div class="hw-panel"><div class="ex-card"><h4>Examples & Practice 💬</h4><div class="ex-item">1) A <span class="vt-word">tidy<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>tidy</b><br>опрятный, аккуратный</span></span> room reduces stress and improves concentration because a clear space reflects a clear mind. 🌟</div><div class="ex-item">2) The kitchen floor is completely <span class="vt-word">clean<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>clean</b><br>чистый</span></span> after scrubbing, smelling fresh of lemon detergent and free from sticky spills. 🍋</div><div class="ex-item">3) If the task remains <span class="vt-word">incomplete<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>incomplete</b><br>незавершённый</span></span>, you'll feel a lingering sense of guilt and will likely procrastinate even more tomorrow. ⏳</div><div class="ex-item">4) The porch looks <span class="vt-word">unswept<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>unswept</b><br>неподметённый</span></span> and dusty after the windy weekend, so grab the broom and clear the debris away. 🍂</div><div class="ex-item">5) A <span class="vt-word">disorderly<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>disorderly</b><br>неорганизованный, беспорядочный</span></span> desk makes it nearly impossible to find important documents when you need them quickly. 📄</div><div class="ex-item">6) This cleaning method isn't <span class="vt-word">perfect<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>perfect</b><br>идеальный, безупречный</span></span>, but it gets the job done efficiently enough for daily maintenance. ✅</div><div class="ex-item">7) Never leave chemical products open around children or pets, as it is highly <span class="vt-word">unsafe<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>unsafe</b><br>небезопасный</span></span> and could lead to serious poisoning accidents. ⚠️</div><div class="ex-item">8) The current supply of sponges is <span class="vt-word">insufficient<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>insufficient</b><br>недостаточный</span></span> for the weekend cleanup, so we need to buy a larger pack before starting. 📦</div><div class="ex-item">9) I finally found the <span class="vt-word">misplaced<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>misplaced</b><br>потерянный, помещённый не туда</span></span> screwdriver under the sofa cushions after searching the entire house for two hours. 🔍</div><div class="ex-item">10) Always wash your hands immediately after handling <span class="vt-word">unwashed<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>unwashed</b><br>невымытый</span></span> produce or raw meat to prevent cross-contamination in the kitchen. 🥦</div></div><div class="practice-box"><h4>🎯 Practice Text: Clean Room 📝</h4><div class="practice-text">A <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">tidy 🌟</span><span class="tooltip">📌 опрятный</span></span> space feels much better. After cleaning, it's completely <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">clean 🍋</span><span class="tooltip">📌 чистый</span></span>. An <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">incomplete ⏳</span><span class="tooltip">📌 незавершённый</span></span> job feels frustrating. If the floor is <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">unswept 🍂</span><span class="tooltip">📌 неподметённый</span></span>, it looks neglected. A <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">disorderly 📄</span><span class="tooltip">📌 неорганизованный</span></span> room wastes time searching. Nothing is <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">perfect ✅</span><span class="tooltip">📌 идеальный</span></span>, but effort matters. Keeping chemicals out of reach is <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">safe ⚠️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 безопасный</span></span>. Don't let supplies become <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">insufficient 📦</span><span class="tooltip">📌 недостаточный</span></span>. If you find a <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">misplaced 🔍</span><span class="tooltip">📌 потерянный</span></span> item, return it. Never touch <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">unwashed 🥦</span><span class="tooltip">📌 невымытый</span></span> food without washing hands first!</div></div></div><button class="hw-btn test" style="background:#0e7490;color:#fff" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">🧪 Test</button><div class="hw-panel"><h3 class="qz-title">🧪 Test: Adjectives</h3><p class="qz-sub">Choose the correct option:</p><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">1</div><div class="qz-sent">She's always ___; she arrives 10 minutes early to every meeting.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q1" id="hw_adj_q1a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adj_q1a" class="qz-opt">A) punctual</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q1" id="hw_adj_q1b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q1b" class="qz-opt">B) unpunctual</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q1" id="hw_adj_q1c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q1c" class="qz-opt">C) unrealistic</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Punctual</b> = пунктуальный, вовремя.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ unpunctual = непунктуальный. unrealistic = нереалистичный.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">2</div><div class="qz-sent">His reply was ___; we needed an answer yesterday.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q2" id="hw_adj_q2a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q2a" class="qz-opt">A) timely</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q2" id="hw_adj_q2b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adj_q2b" class="qz-opt">B) untimely</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q2" id="hw_adj_q2c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q2c" class="qz-opt">C) productive</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Untimely</b> = несвоевременный.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ timely = своевременный. productive = продуктивный.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">3</div><div class="qz-sent">With a clear plan, your study sessions become much ___.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q3" id="hw_adj_q3a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adj_q3a" class="qz-opt">A) productive</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q3" id="hw_adj_q3b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q3b" class="qz-opt">B) unproductive</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q3" id="hw_adj_q3c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q3c" class="qz-opt">C) unreliable</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Productive</b> = продуктивный, результативный.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ unproductive = непродуктивный. unreliable = ненадёжный.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">4</div><div class="qz-sent">The old system was ___; it took twice as long to complete tasks.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q4" id="hw_adj_q4a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q4a" class="qz-opt">A) efficient</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q4" id="hw_adj_q4b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adj_q4b" class="qz-opt">B) inefficient</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q4" id="hw_adj_q4c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q4c" class="qz-opt">C) organized</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Inefficient</b> = неэффективный.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ efficient = эффективный. organized = организованный.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">5</div><div class="qz-sent">His desk is always ___; papers are everywhere.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q5" id="hw_adj_q5a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adj_q5a" class="qz-opt">A) disorganized</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q5" id="hw_adj_q5b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q5b" class="qz-opt">B) organized</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q5" id="hw_adj_q5c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q5c" class="qz-opt">C) punctual</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Disorganized</b> = неорганизованный, в беспорядке.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ organized = организованный. punctual = пунктуальный.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">6</div><div class="qz-sent">Don't be ___; if you promise to call, do it.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q6" id="hw_adj_q6a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q6a" class="qz-opt">A) reliable</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q6" id="hw_adj_q6b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adj_q6b" class="qz-opt">B) unreliable</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q6" id="hw_adj_q6c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q6c" class="qz-opt">C) flexible</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Unreliable</b> = ненадёжный.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ reliable = надёжный. flexible = гибкий.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">7</div><div class="qz-sent">The schedule is ___; we can't change the meeting time.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q7" id="hw_adj_q7a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q7a" class="qz-opt">A) flexible</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q7" id="hw_adj_q7b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adj_q7b" class="qz-opt">B) inflexible</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q7" id="hw_adj_q7c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q7c" class="qz-opt">C) patient</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Inflexible</b> = негибкий, неуступчивый.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ flexible = гибкий. patient = терпеливый.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">8</div><div class="qz-sent">He gets angry when things take time; he's very ___.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q8" id="hw_adj_q8a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q8a" class="qz-opt">A) patient</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q8" id="hw_adj_q8b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adj_q8b" class="qz-opt">B) impatient</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q8" id="hw_adj_q8c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q8c" class="qz-opt">C) realistic</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Impatient</b> = нетерпеливый.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ patient = терпеливый. realistic = реалистичный.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">9</div><div class="qz-sent">Expecting to learn a language in a week is ___.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q9" id="hw_adj_q9a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adj_q9a" class="qz-opt">A) unrealistic</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q9" id="hw_adj_q9b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q9b" class="qz-opt">B) realistic</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q9" id="hw_adj_q9c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q9c" class="qz-opt">C) regular</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Unrealistic</b> = нереалистичный.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ realistic = реалистичный. regular = регулярный.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">10</div><div class="qz-sent">His attendance has been ___ lately; he misses every other class.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q10" id="hw_adj_q10a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q10a" class="qz-opt">A) regular</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q10" id="hw_adj_q10b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adj_q10b" class="qz-opt">B) irregular</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q10" id="hw_adj_q10c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q10c" class="qz-opt">C) productive</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Irregular</b> = нерегулярный.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ regular = регулярный. productive = продуктивный.</div></div></div></div></div></div>

class="ex-card"><h4>Examples & Practice 💬</h4><div class="ex-item">1) Roommates should learn to <span class="vt-word">share responsibilities<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>share responsibilities</b><br>распределять обязанности</span></span> equally, otherwise one person will quickly become overwhelmed and resentful. 🤝</div><div class="ex-item">2) Learning how to <span class="vt-word">keep house<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>keep house</b><br>вести хозяйство</span></span> effectively takes practice, especially when balancing work, study, and social life. 🏡</div><div class="ex-item">3) Regular vacuuming and mopping helps <span class="vt-word">maintain cleanliness<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>maintain cleanliness</b><br>поддерживать чистоту</span></span> in high-traffic areas where dirt accumulates rapidly. 🧼</div><div class="ex-item">4) If you spill coffee, immediately <span class="vt-word">clear the mess<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>clear the mess</b><br>убрать беспорядок</span></span> <span class="prep">before</span> it stains the fabric or damages the wooden table underneath. ☕</div><div class="ex-item">5) It takes courage to <span class="vt-word">tackle clutter<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>tackle clutter</b><br>разобрать завалы</span></span> <span class="prep">in</span> the attic or garage, but you'll be amazed at how much usable space you'll uncover. 📦</div><div class="ex-item">6) Always <span class="vt-word">sort laundry<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>sort laundry</b><br>сортировать бельё</span></span> by fabric weight and color temperature <span class="prep">before</span> starting the washing cycle. 👕</div><div class="ex-item">7) After drying, gently <span class="vt-word">fold clothes<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>fold clothes</b><br>складывать одежду</span></span> neatly to avoid deep wrinkles and make drawer organization much easier. 📏</div><div class="ex-item">8) Use a damp cloth to <span class="vt-word">wipe surfaces<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>wipe surfaces</b><br>протирать поверхности</span></span> <span class="prep">after</span> cooking, removing grease splatters and food crumbs that attract insects. 🍳</div><div class="ex-item">9) Apply baking soda paste and let it sit to <span class="vt-word">scrub stains<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>scrub stains</b><br>оттирать пятна</span></span> <span class="prep">from</span> grout lines without scratching delicate bathroom tiles. 🧽</div><div class="ex-item">10) Children should learn to <span class="vt-word">put things away<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>put things away</b><br>убирать вещи на место</span></span> <span class="prep">after</span> playtime, as it teaches discipline and respect for shared living spaces. 🧸</div></div><div class="practice-box"><h4>🎯 Practice Text: Mastering Chores 🌟</h4><div class="practice-text">Living well means learning to <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">share responsibilities 🤝</span><span class="tooltip">📌 распределять обязанности</span></span>. Everyone should know how to <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">keep house 🏡</span><span class="tooltip">📌 вести хозяйство</span></span> and <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">maintain cleanliness 🧼</span><span class="tooltip">📌 поддерживать чистоту</span></span>. When accidents happen, quickly <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">clear the mess ☕</span><span class="tooltip">📌 убрать беспорядок</span></span>. Don't fear to <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">tackle clutter 📦</span><span class="tooltip">📌 разобрать завалы</span></span>. Always <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">sort laundry 👕</span><span class="tooltip">📌 сортировать бельё</span></span> and <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">fold clothes 📏</span><span class="tooltip">📌 складывать одежду</span></span> carefully. After meals, <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">wipe surfaces 🍳</span><span class="tooltip">📌 протирать поверхности</span></span>. For tough spots, gently <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">scrub stains 🧽</span><span class="tooltip">📌 оттирать пятна</span></span>. Finally, teach kids to <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">put things away 🧸</span><span class="tooltip">📌 убирать вещи</span></span>. Consistency builds a happy home!</div></div></div><button class="hw-btn test" style="background:#65a30d;color:#fff" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">🧪 Test</button><div class="hw-panel"><h3 class="qz-title">🧪 Test: Collocations</h3><p class="qz-sub">Choose the correct option:</p><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">1</div><div class="qz-sent">Roommates should learn to ___ equally.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q1" id="hw_col_q1a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_col_q1a" class="qz-opt">A) share responsibilities</label><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q1" id="hw_col_q1b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_col_q1b" class="qz-opt">B) share responsibility</label><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q1" id="hw_col_q1c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_col_q1c" class="qz-opt">C) share the responsibilities</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Share responsibilities</b> = распределять обязанности.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ share responsibility = единственное число. share the responsibilities = лишний артикль.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">2</div><div class="qz-sent">Learning how to ___ effectively takes practice.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q2" id="hw_col_q2a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_col_q2a" class="qz-opt">A) keep house</label><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q2" id="hw_col_q2b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_col_q2b" class="qz-opt">B) keep the house</label><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q2" id="hw_col_q2c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_col_q2c" class="qz-opt">C) keep a house</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Keep house</b> = вести хозяйство.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ keep the house = содержать дом. keep a house = иметь дом.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">3</div><div class="qz-sent">Regular vacuuming helps ___ in high-traffic areas.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q3" id="hw_col_q3a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_col_q3a" class="qz-opt">A) maintain cleanliness</label><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q3" id="hw_col_q3b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_col_q3b" class="qz-opt">B) maintain the cleanliness</label><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q3" id="hw_col_q3c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_col_q3c" class="qz-opt">C) maintain a cleanliness</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Maintain cleanliness</b> = поддерживать чистоту.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ maintain the cleanliness = лишний артикль. maintain a cleanliness = неверно.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">4</div><div class="qz-sent">If you spill coffee, immediately ___ before it stains.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q4" id="hw_col_q4a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_col_q4a" class="qz-opt">A) clear the mess</label><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q4" id="hw_col_q4b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_col_q4b" class="qz-opt">B) clear mess</label><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q4" id="hw_col_q4c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_col_q4c" class="qz-opt">C) clear a mess</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Clear the mess</b> = убрать беспорядок.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ clear mess = пропущен артикль. clear a mess = неопределённый артикль.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">5</div><div class="qz-sent">It takes courage to ___ in the attic or garage.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q5" id="hw_col_q5a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_col_q5a" class="qz-opt">A) tackle clutter</label><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q5" id="hw_col_q5b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_col_q5b" class="qz-opt">B) tackle the clutter</label><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q5" id="hw_col_q5c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_col_q5c" class="qz-opt">C) tackle a clutter</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Fold clothes</b> = складывать одежду.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ fold the clothes = определённый артикль. fold a clothes = неверно.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">8</div><div class="qz-sent">Use a damp cloth to ___ after cooking.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q8" id="hw_col_q8a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_col_q8a" class="qz-opt">A) wipe surfaces</label><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q8" id="hw_col_q8b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_col_q8b" class="qz-opt">B) wipe the surfaces</label><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q8" id="hw_col_q8c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_col_q8c" class="qz-opt">C) wipe a surfaces</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Wipe surfaces</b> = протирать поверхности.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ wipe the surfaces = определённый артикль. wipe a surfaces = неверно.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">9</div><div class="qz-sent">Apply baking soda paste to ___ from grout lines.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q9" id="hw_col_q9a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_col_q9a" class="qz-opt">A) scrub stains</label><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q9" id="hw_col_q9b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_col_q9b" class="qz-opt">B) scrub the stains</label><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q9" id="hw_col_q9c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_col_q9c" class="qz-opt">C) scrub a stains</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Scrub stains</b> = оттирать пятна.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ scrub the stains = определённый артикль. scrub a stains = неверно.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">10</div><div class="qz-sent">Children should learn to ___ after playtime.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q10" id="hw_col_q10a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_col_q10a" class="qz-opt">A) put things away</label><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q10" id="hw_col_q10b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_col_q10b" class="qz-opt">B) put away things</label><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q10" id="hw_col_q10c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_col_q10c" class="qz-opt">C) put the things away</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Put things away</b> = убирать вещи на место.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ put away things = неверный порядок. put the things away = лишний артикль.</div></div></div></div></div></div>

translateY(5px)}.blur-wrapper:hover .tooltip{transform:translateX(0) translateY(0)}}</style><div class="hw-wrap"><div class="hw-box"><h2 class="hw-title">🧹 Housework & Cleaning: 🎭 Idioms</h2><button class="hw-btn" style="background:#6366f1;color:#fff" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">📘 Learn</button><div class="hw-panel"><div class="ex-card"><h4>Examples & Practice 💬</h4><div class="ex-item">1) "A place for everything and everything in its place." — Keeping items organized saves time and reduces daily stress when looking for keys or documents. 📍<div class="accordion"><div class="accordion-header" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('active')">📖 Show origin/meaning</div><div class="accordion-content">Старая поговорка, подчёркивающая важность порядка и системности. Каждой вещи должно быть своё место, что упрощает жизнь и поиск предметов.</div></div></div><div class="ex-item">2) "I want to start with a <span class="vt-word">clean slate<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>clean slate</b><br>чистый лист, начать заново</span></span> this year." — I'm decluttering my home and adopting new habits to feel refreshed and motivated. 📜<div class="accordion"><div class="accordion-header" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('active')">📖 Show origin/meaning</div><div class="accordion-content">Из школьных времён: ученики стирали грифель с доски ("slate"), чтобы начать урок заново. Означает начать что-то с нуля, без старых проблем.</div></div></div><div class="ex-item">3) "Scrubbing this floor takes serious <span class="vt-word">elbow grease<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>elbow grease</b><br>физические усилия, старание</span></span>." — No modern machine replaces manual effort for deeply embedded dirt. 💪<div class="accordion"><div class="accordion-header" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('active')">📖 Show origin/meaning</div><div class="accordion-content">Идиома 18 века: "elbow grease" означает интенсивную физическую работу руками (трение, оттирание). Подчёркивает, что некоторые задачи требуют усилий.</div></div></div><div class="ex-item">4) "He needs to <span class="vt-word">tidy up his act<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>tidy up one's act</b><br>взяться за ум, исправить поведение</span></span> before he loses his job." — Chronic lateness is unacceptable in a professional environment. 🛠️<div class="accordion"><div class="accordion-header" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('active')">📖 Show origin/meaning</div><div class="accordion-content">Переносное значение: навести порядок не только в вещах, но и в поведении/привычках. Используется при серьёзных предупреждениях.</div></div></div><div class="ex-item">5) "When guests arrive unexpectedly, everyone <span class="vt-word">pitches in<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>pitch in</b><br>подключиться, помочь</span></span> to prepare food and arrange seating." — Teamwork makes emergencies manageable. 🤝<div class="accordion"><div class="accordion-header" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('active')">📖 Show origin/meaning</div><div class="accordion-content">Означать объединять усилия для общего дела. Часто используется в контексте уборки, готовки или решения внезапных задач.</div></div></div><div class="ex-item">6) "If you <span class="vt-word">pull your weight<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>pull one's weight</b><br>делать свою долю работы</span></span>, the household runs smoothly without anyone feeling overworked." ⚖️<div class="accordion"><div class="accordion-header" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('active')">📖 Show origin/meaning</div><div class="accordion-content">Из морского сленга: тянуть свой вес на веслах. Означает честно выполнять свою часть работы в коллективе или семье.</div></div></div><div class="ex-item">7) "We decided to <span class="vt-word">set the house in order<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>set the house in order</b><br>привести дела в порядок</span></span> after the renovation was finally complete." — Organizing paperwork, schedules, and storage systems. 🏠<div class="accordion"><div class="accordion-header" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('active')">📖 Show origin/meaning</div><div class="accordion-content">Означает систематизировать дела, финансы или физическое пространство. Часто используется перед важными событиями или этапами жизни.</div></div></div><div class="ex-item">8) "After the argument, they decided to <span class="vt-word">clean up their act<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>clean up one's act</b><br>исправить поведение</span></span> and communicate respectfully moving forward." 🕊️<div class="accordion"><div class="accordion-header" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('active')">📖 Show origin/meaning</div><div class="accordion-content">Аналогично "tidy up one's act": кардинально изменить поведение, стать более ответственным и дисциплинированным.</div></div></div><div class="ex-item">9) "The new manager did a <span class="vt-word">clean sweep<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>do a clean sweep</b><br>полностью очистить, изменить всё к лучшему</span></span> of outdated policies." — Efficiency improved dramatically. 🌬️<div class="accordion"><div class="accordion-header" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('active')">📖 Show origin/meaning</div><div class="accordion-content">Полное устранение старого/плохого. В уборке — вымести всё до чистоты. В жизни/работе — радикальные позитивные перемены.</div></div></div><div class="ex-item">10) "Apologize sincerely and <span class="vt-word">wipe the slate clean<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>wipe the slate clean</b><br>забыть прошлое, начать заново</span></span> so we can move forward as friends." 🤝<div class="accordion"><div class="accordion-header" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('active')">📖 Show origin/meaning</div><div class="accordion-content">Означает простить обиды, закрыть старые конфликты и дать отношениям или проекту новый старт без груза прошлого.</div></div></div></div><div class="practice-box"><h4>🎯 Practice Text: Life Lessons 📖</h4><div class="practice-text">Believe in "a place for everything and everything in its place 📍". Start each project with a <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">clean slate 📜</span><span class="tooltip">📌 чистый лист</span></span>. Hard work requires real <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">elbow grease 💪</span><span class="tooltip">📌 физические усилия</span></span>. If you make mistakes, it's time to <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">tidy up your act 🛠️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 взяться за ум</span></span>. When others struggle, always <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">pitch in 🤝</span><span class="tooltip">📌 помочь</span></span>. Make sure you <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">pull your weight ⚖️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 делать свою долю</span></span>. Before big changes, <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">set the house in order 🏠</span><span class="tooltip">📌 привести дела в порядок</span></span>. Fix bad habits to <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">clean up your act 🕊️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 исправить поведение</span></span>. Leaders often do a <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">clean sweep 🌬️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 полное обновление</span></span>. Finally, learn to <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">wipe the slate clean 🤝</span><span class="tooltip">📌 забыть прошлое</span></span>. Growth comes from order and forgiveness!</div></div></div><button class="hw-btn test" style="background:#4f46e5;color:#fff" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">🧪 Test</button><div class="hw-panel"><h3 class="qz-title">🧪 Test: Idioms</h3><p class="qz-sub">Choose the correct option:</p><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">1</div><div class="qz-sent">Believe in "___" — keeping items organized saves time.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q1" id="hw_idiom_q1a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_idiom_q1a" class="qz-opt">A) a place for everything and everything in its place</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q1" id="hw_idiom_q1b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q1b" class="qz-opt">B) a place for everything</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q1" id="hw_idiom_q1c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q1c" class="qz-opt">C) everything in its place</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>A place for everything and everything in its place</b> = каждой вещи своё место.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ неполная версия пословицы.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">2</div><div class="qz-sent">I want to start with a ___ this year and adopt new habits.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q2" id="hw_idiom_q2a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_idiom_q2a" class="qz-opt">A) clean slate</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q2" id="hw_idiom_q2b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q2b" class="qz-opt">B) clean start</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q2" id="hw_idiom_q2c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q2c" class="qz-opt">C) fresh slate</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Clean slate</b> = чистый лист, начать заново.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ clean start = не идиома. fresh slate = неверно.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">3</div><div class="qz-sent">Scrubbing this floor takes serious ___ — no machine can replace manual effort.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q3" id="hw_idiom_q3a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_idiom_q3a" class="qz-opt">A) elbow grease</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q3" id="hw_idiom_q3b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q3b" class="qz-opt">B) hard work</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q3" id="hw_idiom_q3c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q3c" class="qz-opt">C) effort</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Elbow grease</b> = физические усилия, старание.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ hard work = не идиома. effort = усилие.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">4</div><div class="qz-sent">He needs to ___ before he loses his job — chronic lateness is unacceptable.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q4" id="hw_idiom_q4a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_idiom_q4a" class="qz-opt">A) tidy up his act</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q4" id="hw_idiom_q4b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q4b" class="qz-opt">B) clean up his act</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q4" id="hw_idiom_q4c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q4c" class="qz-opt">C) fix his act</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Tidy up one's act</b> = взяться за ум, исправить поведение.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ clean up his act = другая идиома. fix his act = неверно.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">5</div><div class="qz-sent">When guests arrive unexpectedly, everyone ___ to prepare food and arrange seating.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q5" id="hw_idiom_q5a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_idiom_q5a" class="qz-opt">A) pitches in</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q5" id="hw_idiom_q5b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q5b" class="qz-opt">B) helps out</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q5" id="hw_idiom_q5c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q5c" class="qz-opt">C) joins in</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Pitch in</b> = подключиться, помочь.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ helps out = помогать. joins in = присоединиться.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">6</div><div class="qz-sent">If you ___, the household runs smoothly without anyone feeling overworked.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q6" id="hw_idiom_q6a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_idiom_q6a" class="qz-opt">A) pull your weight</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q6" id="hw_idiom_q6b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q6b" class="qz-opt">B) do your part</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q6" id="hw_idiom_q6c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q6c" class="qz-opt">C) do your share</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Pull one's weight</b> = делать свою долю работы.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ do your part = делать свою часть. do your share = делать свою долю.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">7</div><div class="qz-sent">We decided to ___ after the renovation was finally complete.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q7" id="hw_idiom_q7a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_idiom_q7a" class="qz-opt">A) set the house in order</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q7" id="hw_idiom_q7b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q7b" class="qz-opt">B) organize the house</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q7" id="hw_idiom_q7c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q7c" class="qz-opt">C) put the house in order</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Set the house in order</b> = привести дела в порядок.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ organize the house = организовать дом. put the house in order = неверно.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">8</div><div class="qz-sent">After the argument, they decided to ___ and communicate respectfully.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q8" id="hw_idiom_q8a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_idiom_q8a" class="qz-opt">A) clean up their act</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q8" id="hw_idiom_q8b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q8b" class="qz-opt">B) tidy up their act</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q8" id="hw_idiom_q8c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q8c" class="qz-opt">C) fix their act</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Clean up one's act</b> = исправить поведение.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ tidy up their act = навести порядок. fix their act = исправить.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">9</div><div class="qz-sent">The new manager did a ___ of outdated policies — efficiency improved dramatically.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q9" id="hw_idiom_q9a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_idiom_q9a" class="qz-opt">A) clean sweep</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q9" id="hw_idiom_q9b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q9b" class="qz-opt">B) full sweep</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q9" id="hw_idiom_q9c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q9c" class="qz-opt">C) complete sweep</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Do a clean sweep</b> = полностью очистить, изменить всё к лучшему.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ full sweep = полная уборка. complete sweep = завершённая уборка.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">10</div><div class="qz-sent">Apologize sincerely and ___ so we can move forward as friends.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q10" id="hw_idiom_q10a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_idiom_q10a" class="qz-opt">A) wipe the slate clean</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q10" id="hw_idiom_q10b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q10b" class="qz-opt">B) clean the slate</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q10" id="hw_idiom_q10c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q10c" class="qz-opt">C) wipe the slate</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Wipe the slate clean</b> = забыть прошлое, начать заново.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ clean the slate = очистить доску. wipe the slate = стереть доску.</div></div></div></div></div></div>

!important;max-width:280px}.blur-wrapper:hover .tooltip{opacity:1;transform:translateX(-50%) translateY(0)}.qz-title{font-size:20px;font-weight:800;color:#0f172a;margin:0 0 5px
