translateY(5px)}.blur-wrapper:hover .tooltip{transform:translateX(0) translateY(0)}}</style><div class="hw-wrap"><div class="hw-box"><h2 class="hw-title">🧹 Housework & Cleaning: 🇬🇧🇺 British vs American</h2><button class="hw-btn" style="background:#f43f5e;color:#fff" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">📘 Learn</button><div class="hw-panel"><h3>Key Vocabulary Differences</h3><table class="ba-table"><tr><th>🇬🇧 British English</th><th>🇺 American English</th><th>🇷🇺 Translation</th></tr><tr><td class="ba-british">flat</td><td class="ba-american">apartment</td><td>квартира</td></tr><tr><td class="ba-british">lift</td><td class="ba-american">elevator</td><td>лифт</td></tr><tr><td class="ba-british">rubbish</td><td class="ba-american">trash</td><td>мусор</td></tr><tr><td class="ba-british">dustbin</td><td class="ba-american">garbage can</td><td>мусорный бак</td></tr><tr><td class="ba-british">washing-up liquid</td><td class="ba-american">dish soap</td><td>средство для мытья посуды</td></tr><tr><td class="ba-british">tea towel</td><td class="ba-american">dish towel</td><td>кухонное полотенце</td></tr><tr><td class="ba-british">hoover</td><td class="ba-american">vacuum</td><td>пылесос</td></tr><tr><td class="ba-british">wardrobe</td><td class="ba-american">closet</td><td>шкаф (для одежды)</td></tr><tr><td class="ba-british">chest of drawers</td><td class="ba-american">dresser</td><td>комод</td></tr><tr><td class="ba-british">bin liner</td><td class="ba-american">trash bag</td><td>мусорный пакет</td></tr></table><div class="ba-practice"><div class="ba-practice-box british"><div class="ba-practice-title">🇬🇧 British English Practice</div><p style="margin:0;line-height:1.8;color:#495057;font-size:15px">After moving into the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">flat</span><span class="tooltip">📌 квартира</span></span>, I use the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">lift</span><span class="tooltip">📌 лифт</span></span> daily. I take the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">rubbish</span><span class="tooltip">📌 мусор</span></span> out and place it in the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">dustbin</span><span class="tooltip">📌 мусорный бак</span></span>. I always keep <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">washing-up liquid</span><span class="tooltip">📌 средство для посуды</span></span> and a <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">tea towel</span><span class="tooltip">📌 полотенце</span></span> by the sink. I run the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">hoover</span><span class="tooltip">📌 пылесос</span></span> weekly. My clothes go into the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">wardrobe</span><span class="tooltip">📌 шкаф</span></span>, socks in the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">chest of drawers</span><span class="tooltip">📌 комод</span></span>. I change the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">bin liner</span><span class="tooltip">📌 пакет</span></span> daily.</p></div><div class="ba-practice-box american"><div class="ba-practice-title">🇺🇸 American English Practice</div><p style="margin:0;line-height:1.8;color:#495057;font-size:15px">After moving into the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">apartment</span><span class="tooltip">📌 квартира</span></span>, I use the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">elevator</span><span class="tooltip">📌 лифт</span></span> daily. I take the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">trash</span><span class="tooltip">📌 мусор</span></span> out and place it in the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">garbage can</span><span class="tooltip">📌 мусорный бак</span></span>. I always keep <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">dish soap</span><span class="tooltip">📌 средство для посуды</span></span> and a <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">dish towel</span><span class="tooltip">📌 полотенце</span></span> by the sink. I run the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">vacuum</span><span class="tooltip">📌 пылесос</span></span> weekly. My clothes go into the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">closet</span><span class="tooltip">📌 шкаф</span></span>, socks in the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">dresser</span><span class="tooltip">📌 комод</span></span>. I change the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">trash bag</span><span class="tooltip">📌 пакет</span></span> daily.</p></div></div><div class="ba-note">💡 <b>Запомните:</b> Британцы чаще говорят <b>"hoover"</b> (от бренда) для пылесоса, а американцы — <b>"vacuum"</b>. Для мусора британцы используют <b>"rubbish"</b>, а американцы — <b>"trash"</b> или <b>"garbage"</b>. Бытовые мелочи различаются, но оба варианта полностью взаимопонятны.</div></div><button class="hw-btn test" style="background:#e11d48;color:#fff" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">🧪 Test</button><div class="hw-panel"><h3 class="qz-title">🧪 Test: British vs American</h3><p class="qz-sub">Choose the correct option:</p><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">1</div><div class="qz-sent">🇬🇧 I live in a small ___ on the third floor.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q1" id="hw_ba_q1a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_ba_q1a" class="qz-opt">A) flat</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q1" id="hw_ba_q1b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ba_q1b" class="qz-opt">B) apartment</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q1" id="hw_ba_q1c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ba_q1c" class="qz-opt">C) house</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! В UK квартира = <b>flat</b>.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ apartment = US вариант. house = дом.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">2</div><div class="qz-sent">🇺 I live in a small ___ on the third floor.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q2" id="hw_ba_q2a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ba_q2a" class="qz-opt">A) flat</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q2" id="hw_ba_q2b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_ba_q2b" class="qz-opt">B) apartment</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q2" id="hw_ba_q2c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ba_q2c" class="qz-opt">C) house</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! В US квартира = <b>apartment</b>.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ flat = UK вариант. house = дом.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">3</div><div class="qz-sent">🇬🇧 Take the ___ to the fifth floor, please.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q3" id="hw_ba_q3a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_ba_q3a" class="qz-opt">A) lift</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q3" id="hw_ba_q3b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ba_q3b" class="qz-opt">B) elevator</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q3" id="hw_ba_q3c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ba_q3c" class="qz-opt">C) stairs</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! В UK лифт = <b>lift</b>.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ elevator = US вариант. stairs = лестница.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">4</div><div class="qz-sent">🇺🇸 Take the ___ to the fifth floor, please.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q4" id="hw_ba_q4a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ba_q4a" class="qz-opt">A) lift</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q4" id="hw_ba_q4b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_ba_q4b" class="qz-opt">B) elevator</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q4" id="hw_ba_q4c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ba_q4c" class="qz-opt">C) stairs</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! В US лифт = <b>elevator</b>.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ lift = UK вариант. stairs = лестница.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">5</div><div class="qz-sent">🇬🇧 Don't forget to take out the ___. </div><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q5" id="hw_ba_q5a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_ba_q5a" class="qz-opt">A) rubbish</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q5" id="hw_ba_q5b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ba_q5b" class="qz-opt">B) trash</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q5" id="hw_ba_q5c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ba_q5c" class="qz-opt">C) garbage</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! В UK мусор = <b>rubbish</b>.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ trash = US вариант. garbage = US вариант.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">6</div><div class="qz-sent">🇺🇸 Don't forget to take out the ___. </div><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q6" id="hw_ba_q6a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ba_q6a" class="qz-opt">A) rubbish</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q6" id="hw_ba_q6b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_ba_q6b" class="qz-opt">B) trash</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q6" id="hw_ba_q6c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ba_q6c" class="qz-opt">C) litter</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! В US мусор = <b>trash</b>.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ rubbish = UK вариант. litter = мусор на улице.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">7</div><div class="qz-sent">🇬🇧 I need to buy some ___ for washing dishes.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q7" id="hw_ba_q7a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_ba_q7a" class="qz-opt">A) washing-up liquid</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q7" id="hw_ba_q7b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ba_q7b" class="qz-opt">B) dish soap</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q7" id="hw_ba_q7c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ba_q7c" class="qz-opt">C) soap</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! В UK средство для посуды = <b>washing-up liquid</b>.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ dish soap = US вариант. soap = мыло.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">8</div><div class="qz-sent">🇺🇸 I need to buy some ___ for washing dishes.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q8" id="hw_ba_q8a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ba_q8a" class="qz-opt">A) washing-up liquid</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q8" id="hw_ba_q8b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_ba_q8b" class="qz-opt">B) dish soap</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q8" id="hw_ba_q8c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ba_q8c" class="qz-opt">C) soap</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! В US средство для посуды = <b>dish soap</b>.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ washing-up liquid = UK вариант. soap = мыло.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">9</div><div class="qz-sent">🇬🇧 I need to run the ___ over the carpet.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q9" id="hw_ba_q9a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_ba_q9a" class="qz-opt">A) hoover</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q9" id="hw_ba_q9b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ba_q9b" class="qz-opt">B) vacuum</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q9" id="hw_ba_q9c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ba_q9c" class="qz-opt">C) cleaner</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! class="dr-title-nouns">Nouns</h2><button class="dr-btn-nouns learn" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">📘 Learn Constructions</button><div class="dr-panel-nouns"><div class="cf-nouns"><strong>⚠️ mop vs broom</strong><div class="diff"><b>mop</b> — швабра (для влажной уборки пола)<br><b>broom</b> — веник/щётка (для сухой уборки)<div class="examples-box-nouns"><span class="examples-label-nouns">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-nouns"><li>I need a <b>mop</b> to clean the kitchen floor. 🧹</li><li>Use the <b>broom</b> to sweep the dust. 🧹</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-nouns"><strong>⚠️ detergent vs soap</strong><div class="diff"><b>detergent</b> — моющее средство (порошок для стирки, жидкость для посуды)<br><b>soap</b> — мыло (для рук, тела)<div class="examples-box-nouns"><span class="examples-label-nouns">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-nouns"><li>Add <b>detergent</b> to the washing machine. 🧺</li><li>Wash your hands with <b>soap</b>. 🧼</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-nouns"><strong>⚠️ stain vs spot vs mark</strong><div class="diff"><b>stain</b> — трудновыводимое пятно (от вина, кофе, жира)<br><b>spot</b> — маленькое пятно/крапинка (лёгкое)<br><b>mark</b> — след/отметка (от карандаша, руки)<div class="examples-box-nouns"><span class="examples-label-nouns">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-nouns"><li>There's a coffee <b>stain</b> on the carpet. ☕</li><li>I see a small <b>spot</b> on your shirt. 👕</li><li>Don't leave <b>marks</b> on the wall! 🖍️</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-nouns"><strong>⚠️ appliance vs device</strong><div class="diff"><b>appliance</b> — крупная бытовая техника (стиральная машина, холодильник, плита)<br><b>device</b> — устройство, прибор (пылесос, утюг, блендер)<div class="examples-box-nouns"><span class="examples-label-nouns">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-nouns"><li>We bought a new kitchen <b>appliance</b> — a dishwasher. 🍽️</li><li>This <b>device</b> makes cleaning easier. 🔌</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-nouns"><strong>⚠️ mess vs clutter vs chaos</strong><div class="diff"><b>mess</b> — беспорядок, грязь (общее состояние)<br><b>clutter</b> — захламлённость (много вещей разбросано)<br><b>chaos</b> — полный хаос (ничего не найти!)<div class="examples-box-nouns"><span class="examples-label-nouns">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-nouns"><li>The kitchen is a <b>mess</b> after cooking. 🍳</li><li>There's too much <b>clutter</b> on the desk. 📚</li><li>My room is in complete <b>chaos</b>! 😱</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-nouns"><strong>⚠️ chore vs task</strong><div class="diff"><b>chore</b> — рутинная домашняя обязанность (часто неприятная)<br><b>task</b> — задача (может быть любой, не только домашней)<div class="examples-box-nouns"><span class="examples-label-nouns">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-nouns"><li>Washing dishes is my least favourite <b>chore</b>. 🍽️</li><li>I have an important <b>task</b> to complete at work. 💼</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="practice-box-nouns"><h4>🎯 Practice Text: Cleaning Day 🧹</h4><div class="practice-text-nouns">Today is cleaning day! First, I need to sweep the floor with a <span class="mask-wrapper-nouns" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-nouns">broom</span><span class="mask-overlay-nouns"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-nouns">📌 веник</span></span>, then wash it with a <span class="mask-wrapper-nouns" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-nouns">mop</span><span class="mask-overlay-nouns"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-nouns">📌 швабра</span></span>. I'll add some <span class="mask-wrapper-nouns" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-nouns">detergent</span><span class="mask-overlay-nouns"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-nouns">📌 моющее средство</span></span> to the water. There's a terrible coffee <span class="mask-wrapper-nouns" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-nouns">stain</span><span class="mask-overlay-nouns"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-nouns">📌 трудновыводимое пятно</span></span> on the carpet that I need to remove. The living room is in a complete <span class="mask-wrapper-nouns" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-nouns">chaos</span><span class="mask-overlay-nouns"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-nouns">📌 полный хаос</span></span> — there's so much <span class="mask-wrapper-nouns" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-nouns">clutter</span><span class="mask-overlay-nouns"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-nouns">📌 захламлённость</span></span> everywhere! I need to organize everything. Washing the dishes is my least favourite <span class="mask-wrapper-nouns" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-nouns">chore</span><span class="mask-overlay-nouns"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-nouns">📌 обязанность</span></span>, but it's a necessary <span class="mask-wrapper-nouns" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-nouns">task</span><span class="mask-overlay-nouns"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-nouns">📌 задача</span></span>. I'll also need to use the vacuum cleaner — it's a very useful <span class="mask-wrapper-nouns" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-nouns">device</span><span class="mask-overlay-nouns"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-nouns">📌 устройство</span></span>. The washing machine is another important kitchen <span class="mask-wrapper-nouns" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-nouns">appliance</span><span class="mask-overlay-nouns"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-nouns">📌 бытовая техника</span></span>. After cleaning, the house won't be such a <span class="mask-wrapper-nouns" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-nouns">mess</span><span class="mask-overlay-nouns"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-nouns">📌 беспорядок</span></span> anymore!</div></div></div><button class="dr-btn-nouns test" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">✏️ Test: Nouns</button><div class="dr-panel-nouns"><h3 class="qz-title-nouns">Test: Nouns</h3><p class="qz-sub-nouns">Choose the correct option:</p><div class="qz-card-nouns"><div class="qz-num-nouns">1</div><div class="qz-sent-nouns">I need a ___ to wash the kitchen floor.</div><input type="radio" name="n_q1" id="n_q1a" class="qz-r-nouns corr"><label for="n_q1a" class="qz-opt-nouns">A) mop</label><input type="radio" name="n_q1" id="n_q1b" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q1b" class="qz-opt-nouns">B) broom</label><input type="radio" name="n_q1" id="n_q1c" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q1c" class="qz-opt-nouns">C) vacuum</label><div class="qz-fb-nouns ok">✅ Correct! <b>Mop</b> = швабра (для влажной уборки пола).</div><div class="qz-fb-nouns no">❌ B) Broom = веник (для сухой уборки). C) Vacuum = пылесос (не для мытья).</div></div><div class="qz-card-nouns"><div class="qz-num-nouns">2</div><div class="qz-sent-nouns">Add some ___ to the washing machine.</div><input type="radio" name="n_q2" id="n_q2a" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q2a" class="qz-opt-nouns">A) soap</label><input type="radio" name="n_q2" id="n_q2b" class="qz-r-nouns corr"><label for="n_q2b" class="qz-opt-nouns">B) detergent</label><input type="radio" name="n_q2" id="n_q2c" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q2c" class="qz-opt-nouns">C) water</label><div class="qz-fb-nouns ok">✅ Correct! <b>Detergent</b> = моющее средство (порошок для стирки).</div><div class="qz-fb-nouns no">❌ A) Soap = мыло (для рук, не для стиральной машины). C) Water = вода (но нужно именно средство).</div></div><div class="qz-card-nouns"><div class="qz-num-nouns">3</div><div class="qz-sent-nouns">There's a terrible coffee ___ on the carpet!</div><input type="radio" name="n_q3" id="n_q3a" class="qz-r-nouns corr"><label for="n_q3a" class="qz-opt-nouns">A) stain</label><input type="radio" name="n_q3" id="n_q3b" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q3b" class="qz-opt-nouns">B) spot</label><input type="radio" name="n_q3" id="n_q3c" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q3c" class="qz-opt-nouns">C) mark</label><div class="qz-fb-nouns ok">✅ Correct! <b>Stain</b> = трудновыводимое пятно (от кофе, вина).</div><div class="qz-fb-nouns no">❌ B) Spot = маленькое лёгкое пятно. C) Mark = след/отметка (от карандаша).</div></div><div class="qz-card-nouns"><div class="qz-num-nouns">4</div><div class="qz-sent-nouns">The washing machine is an important kitchen ___.</div><input type="radio" name="n_q4" id="n_q4a" class="qz-r-nouns corr"><label for="n_q4a" class="qz-opt-nouns">A) appliance</label><input type="radio" name="n_q4" id="n_q4b" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q4b" class="qz-opt-nouns">B) device</label><input type="radio" name="n_q4" id="n_q4c" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q4c" class="qz-opt-nouns">C) tool</label><div class="qz-fb-nouns ok">✅ Correct! <b>Appliance</b> = крупная бытовая техника (стиральная машина).</div><div class="qz-fb-nouns no">❌ B) Device = мелкое устройство (пылесос, утюг). C) Tool = инструмент (молоток, отвёртка).</div></div><div class="qz-card-nouns"><div class="qz-num-nouns">5</div><div class="qz-sent-nouns">My room is in complete ___ — I can't find anything!</div><input type="radio" name="n_q5" id="n_q5a" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q5a" class="qz-opt-nouns">A) mess</label><input type="radio" name="n_q5" id="n_q5b" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q5b" class="qz-opt-nouns">B) clutter</label><input type="radio" name="n_q5" id="n_q5c" class="qz-r-nouns corr"><label for="n_q5c" class="qz-opt-nouns">C) chaos</label><div class="qz-fb-nouns ok">✅ Correct! <b>Chaos</b> = полный хаос (ничего не найти).</div><div class="qz-fb-nouns no">❌ A) Mess = беспорядок (но не такой сильный). B) Clutter = захламлённость (много вещей).</div></div><div class="qz-card-nouns"><div class="qz-num-nouns">6</div><div class="qz-sent-nouns">There's too much ___ on my desk — I need to organize!</div><input type="radio" name="n_q6" id="n_q6a" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q6a" class="qz-opt-nouns">A) mess</label><input type="radio" name="n_q6" id="n_q6b" class="qz-r-nouns corr"><label for="n_q6b" class="qz-opt-nouns">B) clutter</label><input type="radio" name="n_q6" id="n_q6c" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q6c" class="qz-opt-nouns">C) chaos</label><div class="qz-fb-nouns ok">✅ Correct! <b>Clutter</b> = захламлённость (много вещей разбросано).</div><div class="qz-fb-nouns no">❌ A) Mess = общий беспорядок. C) Chaos = полный хаос (сильнее clutter).</div></div><div class="qz-card-nouns"><div class="qz-num-nouns">7</div><div class="qz-sent-nouns">Washing dishes is my least favourite ___.</div><input type="radio" name="n_q7" id="n_q7a" class="qz-r-nouns corr"><label for="n_q7a" class="qz-opt-nouns">A) chore</label><input type="radio" name="n_q7" id="n_q7b" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q7b" class="qz-opt-nouns">B) task</label><input type="radio" name="n_q7" id="n_q7c" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q7c" class="qz-opt-nouns">C) job</label><div class="qz-fb-nouns ok">✅ Correct! <b>Chore</b> = рутинная домашняя обязанность (часто неприятная).</div><div class="qz-fb-nouns no">❌ B) Task = задача (может быть любой). C) Job = работа (профессиональная).</div></div><div class="qz-card-nouns"><div class="qz-num-nouns">8</div><div class="qz-sent-nouns">I have an important ___ to complete at work.</div><input type="radio" name="n_q8" id="n_q8a" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q8a" class="qz-opt-nouns">A) chore</label><input type="radio" name="n_q8" id="n_q8b" class="qz-r-nouns corr"><label for="n_q8b" class="qz-opt-nouns">B) task</label><input type="radio" name="n_q8" id="n_q8c" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q8c" class="qz-opt-nouns">C) duty</label><div class="qz-fb-nouns ok">✅ Correct! <b>Task</b> = задача (может быть рабочей, не только домашней).</div><div class="qz-fb-nouns no">❌ A) Chore = домашняя обязанность. C) Duty = долг/обязанность (более формально).</div></div><div class="qz-card-nouns"><div class="qz-num-nouns">9</div><div class="qz-sent-nouns">Use the ___ to sweep the dust from the floor.</div><input type="radio" name="n_q9" id="n_q9a" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q9a" class="qz-opt-nouns">A) mop</label><input type="radio" name="n_q9" id="n_q9b" class="qz-r-nouns corr"><label for="n_q9b" class="qz-opt-nouns">B) broom</label><input type="radio" name="n_q9" id="n_q9c" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q9c" class="qz-opt-nouns">C) cloth</label><div class="qz-fb-nouns ok">✅ Correct! <b>Broom</b> = веник/щётка (для сухой уборки, подметания).</div><div class="qz-fb-nouns no">❌ A) Mop = швабра (для влажной уборки). C) Cloth = тряпка (для протирания).</div></div><div class="qz-card-nouns"><div class="qz-num-nouns">10</div><div class="qz-sent-nouns">The kitchen is a ___ after cooking dinner.</div><input type="radio" name="n_q10" id="n_q10a" class="qz-r-nouns corr"><label for="n_q10a" class="qz-opt-nouns">A) mess</label><input type="radio" name="n_q10" id="n_q10b" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q10b" class="qz-opt-nouns">B) clutter</label><input type="radio" name="n_q10" id="n_q10c" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q10c" class="qz-opt-nouns">C) chaos</label><div class="qz-fb-nouns ok">✅ Correct! <b>Mess</b> = беспорядок, грязь (общее состояние после готовки).</div><div class="qz-fb-nouns no">❌ B) Clutter = захламлённость (много вещей). C) Chaos = полный хаос (сильнее mess).</div></div></div></div></div>

class="dr-title-verbs">Verbs</h2><button class="dr-btn-verbs learn" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">📘 Learn Constructions</button><div class="dr-panel-verbs"><div class="cf-verbs"><strong>⚠️ scrub vs wipe vs scour</strong><div class="diff"><b>scrub</b> — тереть с усилием (щеткой, губкой)<br><b>wipe</b> — протирать легко (тряпкой, рукой)<br><b>scour</b> — чистить до блеска, очень интенсивно<div class="examples-box-verbs"><span class="examples-label-verbs">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-verbs"><li>I need to <b>scrub</b> the dirty floor. 🧽</li><li>Just <b>wipe</b> the table with a cloth. 🧻</li><li>She <b>scoured</b> the pan until it shined. ✨</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-verbs"><strong>⚠️ sweep vs mop vs vacuum</strong><div class="diff"><b>sweep</b> — подметать веником/щёткой (сухая уборка)<br><b>mop</b> — мыть шваброй (влажная уборка)<br><b>vacuum</b> — пылесосить<div class="examples-box-verbs"><span class="examples-label-verbs">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-verbs"><li><b>Sweep</b> the crumbs from the floor. 🧹</li><li><b>Mop</b> the kitchen — it's sticky! 💦</li><li>I need to <b>vacuum</b> the carpet. 🔌</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-verbs"><strong>⚠️ dust vs polish</strong><div class="diff"><b>dust</b> — вытирать пыль (с мебели, полок)<br><b>polish</b> — полировать до блеска (мебель, серебро, обувь)<div class="examples-box-verbs"><span class="examples-label-verbs">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-verbs"><li><b>Dust</b> the shelves — they're covered in dust! 🕸️</li><li>He <b>polished</b> his shoes for the interview. 👞</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-verbs"><strong>⚠️ tidy vs organize</strong><div class="diff"><b>tidy</b> — быстро прибрать, разложить вещи по местам<br><b>organize</b> — системно организовать (с планом, категориями)<div class="examples-box-verbs"><span class="examples-label-verbs">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-verbs"><li><b>Tidy</b> your room before guests arrive! 🛏️</li><li>I need to <b>organize</b> my files by date. 📁</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-verbs"><strong>⚠️ fold vs hang</strong><div class="diff"><b>fold</b> — складывать одежду (футболки, джинсы)<br><b>hang</b> — вешать на плечики (рубашки, платья)<div class="examples-box-verbs"><span class="examples-label-verbs">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-verbs"><li><b>Fold</b> the clean laundry. 👕</li><li><b>Hang</b> your dress — it will wrinkle! 👗</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="practice-box-verbs"><h4>🎯 Practice Text: Spring Cleaning 🌸</h4><div class="practice-text-verbs">Today I'm doing spring cleaning! First, I'll <span class="mask-wrapper-verbs" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-verbs">sweep</span><span class="mask-overlay-verbs"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-verbs">📌 подметать</span></span> the floor with a broom to remove all the crumbs. Then I'll <span class="mask-wrapper-verbs" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-verbs">mop</span><span class="mask-overlay-verbs"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-verbs">📌 мыть шваброй</span></span> it with hot water and detergent. The carpet is very dirty, so I need to <span class="mask-wrapper-verbs" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-verbs">vacuum</span><span class="mask-overlay-verbs"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-verbs">📌 пылесосить</span></span> it thoroughly. There's a terrible stain on the pan — I'll have to <span class="mask-wrapper-verbs" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-verbs">scrub</span><span class="mask-overlay-verbs"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-verbs">📌 тереть с усилием</span></span> it hard with a brush! After that, I'll just <span class="mask-wrapper-verbs" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-verbs">wipe</span><span class="mask-overlay-verbs"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-verbs">📌 протирать</span></span> the table with a damp cloth. The shelves are covered in dust, so I need to <span class="mask-wrapper-verbs" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-verbs">dust</span><span class="mask-overlay-verbs"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-verbs">📌 вытирать пыль</span></span> them carefully. My shoes look terrible — I should <span class="mask-wrapper-verbs" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-verbs">polish</span><span class="mask-overlay-verbs"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-verbs">📌 полировать</span></span> them before the party. My room is a mess, so I'll <span class="mask-wrapper-verbs" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-verbs">tidy</span><span class="mask-overlay-verbs"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-verbs">📌 прибрать</span></span> it quickly. But my closet needs serious help — I need to <span class="mask-wrapper-verbs" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-verbs">organize</span><span class="mask-overlay-verbs"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-verbs">📌 организовать</span></span> all my clothes by season. After laundry, I'll <span class="mask-wrapper-verbs" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-verbs">fold</span><span class="mask-overlay-verbs"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-verbs">📌 складывать</span></span> the t-shirts and jeans, and <span class="mask-wrapper-verbs" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-verbs">hang</span><span class="mask-overlay-verbs"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-verbs">📌 вешать</span></span> the dresses and shirts on hangers.</div></div></div><button class="dr-btn-verbs test" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">✏️ Test: Verbs</button><div class="dr-panel-verbs"><h3 class="qz-title-verbs">Test: Verbs</h3><p class="qz-sub-verbs">Choose the correct option:</p><div class="qz-card-verbs"><div class="qz-num-verbs">1</div><div class="qz-sent-verbs">I need to ___ the dirty floor with a brush.</div><input type="radio" name="v_q1" id="v_q1a" class="qz-r-verbs corr"><label for="v_q1a" class="qz-opt-verbs">A) scrub</label><input type="radio" name="v_q1" id="v_q1b" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q1b" class="qz-opt-verbs">B) wipe</label><input type="radio" name="v_q1" id="v_q1c" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q1c" class="qz-opt-verbs">C) dust</label><div class="qz-fb-verbs ok">✅ Correct! <b>Scrub</b> = тереть с усилием (щеткой, губкой).</div><div class="qz-fb-verbs no">❌ B) Wipe = протирать легко (тряпкой). C) Dust = вытирать пыль.</div></div><div class="qz-card-verbs"><div class="qz-num-verbs">2</div><div class="qz-sent-verbs">Just ___ the table with a cloth — it's not that dirty.</div><input type="radio" name="v_q2" id="v_q2a" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q2a" class="qz-opt-verbs">A) scrub</label><input type="radio" name="v_q2" id="v_q2b" class="qz-r-verbs corr"><label for="v_q2b" class="qz-opt-verbs">B) wipe</label><input type="radio" name="v_q2" id="v_q2c" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q2c" class="qz-opt-verbs">C) scour</label><div class="qz-fb-verbs ok">✅ Correct! <b>Wipe</b> = протирать легко (не грязно, достаточно тряпки).</div><div class="qz-fb-verbs no">❌ A) Scrub = тереть с усилием (слишком много для чистого стола). C) Scour = чистить до блеска (очень интенсивно).</div></div><div class="qz-card-verbs"><div class="qz-num-verbs">3</div><div class="qz-sent-verbs">___ the crumbs from the floor with a broom.</div><input type="radio" name="v_q3" id="v_q3a" class="qz-r-verbs corr"><label for="v_q3a" class="qz-opt-verbs">A) Sweep</label><input type="radio" name="v_q3" id="v_q3b" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q3b" class="qz-opt-verbs">B) Mop</label><input type="radio" name="v_q3" id="v_q3c" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q3c" class="qz-opt-verbs">C) Vacuum</label><div class="qz-fb-verbs ok">✅ Correct! <b>Sweep</b> = подметать веником (сухая уборка).</div><div class="qz-fb-verbs no">❌ B) Mop = мыть шваброй (влажная уборка). C) Vacuum = пылесосить (не веником).</div></div><div class="qz-card-verbs"><div class="qz-num-verbs">4</div><div class="qz-sent-verbs">The kitchen floor is sticky — I need to ___ it.</div><input type="radio" name="v_q4" id="v_q4a" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q4a" class="qz-opt-verbs">A) sweep</label><input type="radio" name="v_q4" id="v_q4b" class="qz-r-verbs corr"><label for="v_q4b" class="qz-opt-verbs">B) mop</label><input type="radio" name="v_q4" id="v_q4c" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q4c" class="qz-opt-verbs">C) dust</label><div class="qz-fb-verbs ok">✅ Correct! <b>Mop</b> = мыть шваброй (влажная уборка для липкого пола).</div><div class="qz-fb-verbs no">❌ A) Sweep = подметать (не поможет с липкостью). C) Dust = вытирать пыль (не для пола).</div></div><div class="qz-card-verbs"><div class="qz-num-verbs">5</div><div class="qz-sent-verbs">I need to ___ the carpet — there's so much dust!</div><input type="radio" name="v_q5" id="v_q5a" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q5a" class="qz-opt-verbs">A) sweep</label><input type="radio" name="v_q5" id="v_q5b" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q5b" class="qz-opt-verbs">B) mop</label><input type="radio" name="v_q5" id="v_q5c" class="qz-r-verbs corr"><label for="v_q5c" class="qz-opt-verbs">C) vacuum</label><div class="qz-fb-verbs ok">✅ Correct! <b>Vacuum</b> = пылесосить (для ковра).</div><div class="qz-fb-verbs no">❌ A) Sweep = подметать веником (не для ковра). B) Mop = мыть шваброй (нельзя мыть ковер).</div></div><div class="qz-card-verbs"><div class="qz-num-verbs">6</div><div class="qz-sent-verbs">The shelves are covered in dust — I need to ___ them.</div><input type="radio" name="v_q6" id="v_q6a" class="qz-r-verbs corr"><label for="v_q6a" class="qz-opt-verbs">A) dust</label><input type="radio" name="v_q6" id="v_q6b" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q6b" class="qz-opt-verbs">B) polish</label><input type="radio" name="v_q6" id="v_q6c" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q6c" class="qz-opt-verbs">C) scrub</label><div class="qz-fb-verbs ok">✅ Correct! <b>Dust</b> = вытирать пыль (с мебели, полок).</div><div class="qz-fb-verbs no">❌ B) Polish = полировать до блеска (не нужно для пыли). C) Scrub = тереть с усилием (слишком много).</div></div><div class="qz-card-verbs"><div class="qz-num-verbs">7</div><div class="qz-sent-verbs">He ___ his shoes for the interview — they look brand new!</div><input type="radio" name="v_q7" id="v_q7a" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q7a" class="qz-opt-verbs">A) dusted</label><input type="radio" name="v_q7" id="v_q7b" class="qz-r-verbs corr"><label for="v_q7b" class="qz-opt-verbs">B) polished</label><input type="radio" name="v_q7" id="v_q7c" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q7c" class="qz-opt-verbs">C) wiped</label><div class="qz-fb-verbs ok">✅ Correct! <b>Polished</b> = отполировал до блеска (обувь).</div><div class="qz-fb-verbs no">❌ A) Dusted = вытер пыль (не даст блеска). C) Wiped = протер (не достаточно для блеска).</div></div><div class="qz-card-verbs"><div class="qz-num-verbs">8</div><div class="qz-sent-verbs">___ your room before guests arrive!</div><input type="radio" name="v_q8" id="v_q8a" class="qz-r-verbs corr"><label for="v_q8a" class="qz-opt-verbs">A) Tidy</label><input type="radio" name="v_q8" id="v_q8b" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q8b" class="qz-opt-verbs">B) Organize</label><input type="radio" name="v_q8" id="v_q8c" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q8c" class="qz-opt-verbs">C) Fold</label><div class="qz-fb-verbs ok">✅ Correct! <b>Tidy</b> = быстро прибрать (разложить вещи по местам).</div><div class="qz-fb-verbs no">❌ B) Organize = системно организовать (займёт много времени). C) Fold = складывать (только одежду).</div></div><div class="qz-card-verbs"><div class="qz-num-verbs">9</div><div class="qz-sent-verbs">I need to ___ my files by date and category.</div><input type="radio" name="v_q9" id="v_q9a" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q9a" class="qz-opt-verbs">A) tidy</label><input type="radio" name="v_q9" id="v_q9b" class="qz-r-verbs corr"><label for="v_q9b" class="qz-opt-verbs">B) organize</label><input type="radio" name="v_q9" id="v_q9c" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q9c" class="qz-opt-verbs">C) hang</label><div class="qz-fb-verbs ok">✅ Correct! <b>Organize</b> = системно организовать (с планом, категориями).</div><div class="qz-fb-verbs no">❌ A) Tidy = просто прибрать (без системы). C) Hang = вешать (не подходит для файлов).</div></div><div class="qz-card-verbs"><div class="qz-num-verbs">10</div><div class="qz-sent-verbs">After laundry, I'll ___ the t-shirts and ___ the dresses.</div><input type="radio" name="v_q10" id="v_q10a" class="qz-r-verbs corr"><label for="v_q10a" class="qz-opt-verbs">A) fold / hang</label><input type="radio" name="v_q10" id="v_q10b" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q10b" class="qz-opt-verbs">B) hang / fold</label><input type="radio" name="v_q10" id="v_q10c" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q10c" class="qz-opt-verbs">C) fold / fold</label><div class="qz-fb-verbs ok">✅ Correct! <b>Fold</b> = складывать (футболки), <b>hang</b> = вешать (платья).</div><div class="qz-fb-verbs no">❌ B) Порядок неправильный — футболки складывают, платья вешают. C) Платья нельзя складывать — помнутся!</div></div></div></div></div>

class="dr-title-phrasal">Phrasal Verbs</h2><button class="dr-btn-phrasal learn" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">📘 Learn Constructions</button><div class="dr-panel-phrasal"><div class="cf-phrasal"><strong>⚠️ clean up / tidy up / do up</strong><div class="diff"><b>clean up</b> — убрать грязь, помыть (удалить грязь)<br><b>tidy up</b> — навести порядок, разложить вещи по местам<br><b>do up</b> — прибрать, привести в порядок (разговорное)<div class="examples-box-phrasal"><span class="examples-label-phrasal">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-phrasal"><li>I need to <b>clean up</b> the kitchen after cooking. 🧽</li><li><b>Tidy up</b> your room — clothes everywhere! 👕</li><li>Let's <b>do up</b> the house before guests arrive. 🏠</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-phrasal"><strong>⚠️ put away / put back / put aside</strong><div class="diff"><b>put away</b> — убрать на место (в шкаф, ящик)<br><b>put back</b> — вернуть обратно (откуда взял)<br><b>put aside</b> — отложить в сторону (на потом)<div class="examples-box-phrasal"><span class="examples-label-phrasal">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-phrasal"><li><b>Put away</b> the clean dishes. 🍽️</li><li>I borrowed your book — I'll <b>put it back</b> tomorrow. 📚</li><li><b>Put aside</b> some money for vacation. </li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-phrasal"><strong>️ throw out / throw away / get rid of</strong><div class="diff"><b>throw out</b> — выбросить (в мусор)<br><b>throw away</b> — выбросить (синоним throw out)<br><b>get rid of</b> — избавиться от чего-то (не обязательно выбросить)<div class="examples-box-phrasal"><span class="examples-label-phrasal">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-phrasal"><li><b>Throw out</b> the trash — it smells! 🗑️</li><li>Don't <b>throw away</b> that box — we might need it! 📦</li><li>I need to <b>get rid of</b> these old clothes. </li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-phrasal"><strong>️ wipe down / wipe off / wipe up</strong><div class="diff"><b>wipe down</b> — протереть поверхность (стол, полку)<br><b>wipe off</b> — стереть что-то с поверхности (пятно, грязь)<br><b>wipe up</b> — вытереть жидкость (разлитую воду)<div class="examples-box-phrasal"><span class="examples-label-phrasal">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-phrasal"><li><b>Wipe down</b> the counters after cooking. 🧻</li><li>I spilled coffee — let me <b>wipe it off</b>. ☕</li><li>The floor is wet — <b>wipe it up</b>! 💦</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-phrasal"><strong>⚠️ clear away / clear up / clear off</strong><div class="diff"><b>clear away</b> — убрать со стола (посуду после еды)<br><b>clear up</b> — прибрать, навести порядок (в комнате)<br><b>clear off</b> — очистить поверхность (от вещей)<div class="examples-box-phrasal"><span class="examples-label-phrasal">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-phrasal"><li>Can you <b>clear away</b> the dishes? ️</li><li>I need to <b>clear up</b> my messy desk. 📁</li><li><b>Clear off</b> the table — we need space! 🪑</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-phrasal"><strong>⚠️ sort out / sort through</strong><div class="diff"><b>sort out</b> — рассортировать, разобрать (по категориям)<br><b>sort through</b> — просматривать, перебирать (в поисках чего-то)<div class="examples-box-phrasal"><span class="examples-label-phrasal">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-phrasal"><li>I need to <b>sort out</b> my laundry — whites and colors. 🧺</li><li>I'm <b>sorting through</b> old photos. </li></ul></div></div></div><div class="practice-box-phrasal"><h4>🎯 Practice Text: Weekend Cleaning 🧹</h4><div class="practice-text-phrasal">This weekend I need to <span class="mask-wrapper-phrasal" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-phrasal">clean up</span><span class="mask-overlay-phrasal"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-phrasal">📌 убрать грязь</span></span> the whole house. First, I'll <span class="mask-wrapper-phrasal" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-phrasal">tidy up</span><span class="mask-overlay-phrasal"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-phrasal">📌 навести порядок</span></span> my bedroom — there are clothes everywhere! I need to <span class="mask-wrapper-phrasal" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-phrasal">put away</span><span class="mask-overlay-phrasal"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-phrasal">📌 убрать на место</span></span> all the clean laundry. Then I'll <span class="mask-wrapper-phrasal" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-phrasal">sort out</span><span class="mask-overlay-phrasal"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-phrasal">📌 рассортировать</span></span> the dirty clothes into whites and colors. After breakfast, I'll <span class="mask-wrapper-phrasal" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-phrasal">clear away</span><span class="mask-overlay-phrasal"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-phrasal">📌 убрать со стола</span></span> the dishes and <span class="mask-wrapper-phrasal" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-phrasal">wipe down</span><span class="mask-overlay-phrasal"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-phrasal">📌 протереть</span></span> the kitchen counters. I spilled some juice, so I need to <span class="mask-wrapper-phrasal" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-phrasal">wipe it up</span><span class="mask-overlay-phrasal"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-phrasal">📌 вытереть жидкость</span></span> quickly! There's a stain on my shirt — I'll try to <span class="mask-wrapper-phrasal" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-phrasal">wipe it off</span><span class="mask-overlay-phrasal"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-phrasal">📌 стереть пятно</span></span>. My closet is a disaster — I need to <span class="mask-wrapper-phrasal" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-phrasal">sort through</span><span class="mask-overlay-phrasal"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-phrasal"> перебирать</span></span> all my clothes and <span class="mask-wrapper-phrasal" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-phrasal">get rid of</span><span class="mask-overlay-phrasal"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-phrasal">📌 избавиться от</span></span> the ones I don't wear anymore. I'll <span class="mask-wrapper-phrasal" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-phrasal">throw out</span><span class="mask-overlay-phrasal"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-phrasal"> выбросить</span></span> the torn jeans and <span class="mask-wrapper-phrasal" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-phrasal">put aside</span><span class="mask-overlay-phrasal"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-phrasal">📌 отложить</span></span> the winter clothes for later. Finally, I'll <span class="mask-wrapper-phrasal" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-phrasal">clear up</span><span class="mask-overlay-phrasal"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-phrasal">📌 прибрать</span></span> the living room and <span class="mask-wrapper-phrasal" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-phrasal">clear off</span><span class="mask-overlay-phrasal"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-phrasal">📌 очистить</span></span> the coffee table. The house will look amazing!</div></div></div><button class="dr-btn-phrasal test" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">✏️ Test: Phrasal Verbs</button><div class="dr-panel-phrasal"><h3 class="qz-title-phrasal">Test: Phrasal Verbs</h3><p class="qz-sub-phrasal">Choose the correct option:</p><div class="qz-card-phrasal"><div class="qz-num-phrasal">1</div><div class="qz-sent-phrasal">I need to ___ the kitchen after cooking — it's dirty!</div><input type="radio" name="pv_q1" id="pv_q1a" class="qz-r-phrasal corr"><label for="pv_q1a" class="qz-opt-phrasal">A) clean up</label><input type="radio" name="pv_q1" id="pv_q1b" class="qz-r-phrasal wrng"><label for="pv_q1b" class="qz-opt-phrasal">B) tidy up</label><input type="radio" name="pv_q1" id="pv_q1c" class="qz-r-phrasal wrng"><label for="pv_q1c" class="qz-opt-phrasal">C) clear up</label><div class="qz-fb-phrasal ok">✅ Correct! <b>Clean up</b> = убрать грязь, помыть (удалить грязь).</div><div class="qz-fb-phrasal no">❌ B) Tidy up = навести порядок (разложить вещи). C) Clear up = прибрать комнату (не кухню).</div></div><div class="qz-card-phrasal"><div class="qz-num-phrasal">2</div><div class="qz-sent-phrasal">___ your room — there are clothes everywhere!</div><input type="radio" name="pv_q2" id="pv_q2a" class="qz-r-phrasal wrng"><label for="pv_q2a" class="qz-opt-phrasal">A) Clean up</label><input type="radio" name="pv_q2" id="pv_q2b" class="qz-r-phrasal corr"><label for="pv_q2b" class="qz-opt-phrasal">B) Tidy up</label><input type="radio" name="pv_q2" id="pv_q2c" class="qz-r-phrasal wrng"><label for="pv_q2c" class="qz-opt-phrasal">C) Wipe down</label><div class="qz-fb-phrasal ok">✅ Correct! <b>Tidy up</b> = навести порядок, разложить вещи по местам.</div><div class="qz-fb-phrasal no">❌ A) Clean up = убрать грязь (не про разбросанные вещи). C) Wipe down = протереть поверхность.</div></div><div class="qz-card-phrasal"><div class="qz-num-phrasal">3</div><div class="qz-sent-phrasal">Please ___ the clean dishes in the cupboard.</div><input type="radio" name="pv_q3" id="pv_q3a" class="qz-r-phrasal corr"><label for="pv_q3a" class="qz-opt-phrasal">A) put away</label><input type="radio" name="pv_q3" id="pv_q3b" class="qz-r-phrasal wrng"><label for="pv_q3b" class="qz-opt-phrasal">B) put back</label><input type="radio" name="pv_q3" id="pv_q3c" class="qz-r-phrasal wrng"><label for="pv_q3c" class="qz-opt-phrasal">C) put aside</label><div class="qz-fb-phrasal ok">✅ Correct! <b>Put away</b> = убрать на место (в шкаф, ящик).</div><div class="qz-fb-phrasal no">❌ B) Put back = вернуть обратно (откуда взял). C) Put aside = отложить в сторону.</div></div><div class="qz-card-phrasal"><div class="qz-num-phrasal">4</div><div class="qz-sent-phrasal">I borrowed your book — I'll ___ it ___ tomorrow.</div><input type="radio" name="pv_q4" id="pv_q4a" class="qz-r-phrasal wrng"><label for="pv_q4a" class="qz-opt-phrasal">A) put / away</label><input type="radio" name="pv_q4" id="pv_q4b" class="qz-r-phrasal corr"><label for="pv_q4b" class="qz-opt-phrasal">B) put / back</label><input type="radio" name="pv_q4" id="pv_q4c" class="qz-r-phrasal wrng"><label for="pv_q4c" class="qz-opt-phrasal">C) put / aside</label><div class="qz-fb-phrasal ok">✅ Correct! <b>Put back</b> = вернуть обратно (откуда взял).</div><div class="qz-fb-phrasal no">❌ A) Put away = убрать на место (не возвращать). C) Put aside = отложить в сторону.</div></div><div class="qz-card-phrasal"><div class="qz-num-phrasal">5</div><div class="qz-sent-phrasal">Don't ___ that box — we might need it later!</div><input type="radio" name="pv_q5" id="pv_q5a" class="qz-r-phrasal wrng"><label for="pv_q5a" class="qz-opt-phrasal">A) put aside</label><input type="radio" name="pv_q5" id="pv_q5b" class="qz-r-phrasal corr"><label for="pv_q5b" class="qz-opt-phrasal">B) throw away</label><input type="radio" name="pv_q5" id="pv_q5c" class="qz-r-phrasal wrng"><label for="pv_q5c" class="qz-opt-phrasal">C) sort out</label><div class="qz-fb-phrasal ok">✅ Correct! <b>Throw away</b> = выбросить (в мусор).</div><div class="qz-fb-phrasal no"> A) Put aside = отложить (не выбрасывать). C) Sort out = рассортировать.</div></div><div class="qz-card-phrasal"><div class="qz-num-phrasal">6</div><div class="qz-sent-phrasal">I spilled juice — let me ___ it ___!</div><input type="radio" name="pv_q6" id="pv_q6a" class="qz-r-phrasal wrng"><label for="pv_q6a" class="qz-opt-phrasal">A) wipe / down</label><input type="radio" name="pv_q6" id="pv_q6b" class="qz-r-phrasal wrng"><label for="pv_q6b" class="qz-opt-phrasal">B) wipe / off</label><input type="radio" name="pv_q6" id="pv_q6c" class="qz-r-phrasal corr"><label for="pv_q6c" class="qz-opt-phrasal">C) wipe / up</label><div class="qz-fb-phrasal ok">✅ Correct! <b>Wipe up</b> = вытереть жидкость (разлитую).</div><div class="qz-fb-phrasal no">❌ A) Wipe down = протереть поверхность. B) Wipe off = стереть что-то с поверхности (не жидкость).</div></div><div class="qz-card-phrasal"><div class="qz-num-phrasal">7</div><div class="qz-sent-phrasal">Can you ___ the dishes after dinner?</div><input type="radio" name="pv_q7" id="pv_q7a" class="qz-r-phrasal corr"><label for="pv_q7a" class="qz-opt-phrasal">A) clear away</label><input type="radio" name="pv_q7" id="pv_q7b" class="qz-r-phrasal wrng"><label for="pv_q7b" class="qz-opt-phrasal">B) clear up</label><input type="radio" name="pv_q7" id="pv_q7c" class="qz-r-phrasal wrng"><label for="pv_q7c" class="qz-opt-phrasal">C) clear off</label><div class="qz-fb-phrasal ok">✅ Correct! <b>Clear away</b> = убрать со стола (посуду после еды).</div><div class="qz-fb-phrasal no">❌ B) Clear up = прибрать комнату. C) Clear off = очистить поверхность (от вещей).</div></div><div class="qz-card-phrasal"><div class="qz-num-phrasal">8</div><div class="qz-sent-phrasal">I need to ___ my laundry — whites and colors.</div><input type="radio" name="pv_q8" id="pv_q8a" class="qz-r-phrasal corr"><label for="pv_q8a" class="qz-opt-phrasal">A) sort out</label><input type="radio" name="pv_q8" id="pv_q8b" class="qz-r-phrasal wrng"><label for="pv_q8b" class="qz-opt-phrasal">B) sort through</label><input type="radio" name="pv_q8" id="pv_q8c" class="qz-r-phrasal wrng"><label for="pv_q8c" class="qz-opt-phrasal">C) throw out</label><div class="qz-fb-phrasal ok">✅ Correct! <b>Sort out</b> = рассортировать (по категориям).</div><div class="qz-fb-phrasal no">❌ B) Sort through = просматривать, перебирать (в поисках). C) Throw out = выбросить.</div></div><div class="qz-card-phrasal"><div class="qz-num-phrasal">9</div><div class="qz-sent-phrasal">I'm ___ old photos — looking for my wedding pictures.</div><input type="radio" name="pv_q9" id="pv_q9a" class="qz-r-phrasal wrng"><label for="pv_q9a" class="qz-opt-phrasal">A) sorting out</label><input type="radio" name="pv_q9" id="pv_q9b" class="qz-r-phrasal corr"><label for="pv_q9b" class="qz-opt-phrasal">B) sorting through</label><input type="radio" name="pv_q9" id="pv_q9c" class="qz-r-phrasal wrng"><label for="pv_q9c" class="qz-opt-phrasal">C) getting rid of</label><div class="qz-fb-phrasal ok">✅ Correct! <b>Sorting through</b> = просматривать, перебирать (в поисках чего-то).</div><div class="qz-fb-phrasal no">❌ A) Sorting out = рассортировать (по категориям). C) Getting rid of = избавляться от.</div></div><div class="qz-card-phrasal"><div class="qz-num-phrasal">10</div><div class="qz-sent-phrasal">I need to ___ these old clothes — I never wear them!</div><input type="radio" name="pv_q10" id="pv_q10a" class="qz-r-phrasal wrng"><label for="pv_q10a" class="qz-opt-phrasal">A) put away</label><input type="radio" name="pv_q10" id="pv_q10b" class="qz-r-phrasal wrng"><label for="pv_q10b" class="qz-opt-phrasal">B) sort out</label><input type="radio" name="pv_q10" id="pv_q10c" class="qz-r-phrasal corr"><label for="pv_q10c" class="qz-opt-phrasal">C) get rid of</label><div class="qz-fb-phrasal ok">✅ Correct! <b>Get rid of</b> = избавиться от чего-то.</div><div class="qz-fb-phrasal no">❌ A) Put away = убрать на место. B) Sort out = рассортировать.</div></div></div></div></div>

class="dr-wrap-expressions"><h2 class="dr-title-expressions">Expressions</h2><button class="dr-btn-expressions learn" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">📘 Learn Constructions</button><div class="dr-panel-expressions"><div class="cf-expressions"><strong>⚠️ set the table vs clear the table</strong><div class="diff"><b>set the table</b> — накрыть на стол (поставить тарелки, приборы)<br><b>clear the table</b> — убрать со стола (посуду после еды)<div class="examples-box-expressions"><span class="examples-label-expressions">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-expressions"><li>Please <b>set the table</b> before dinner. 🍽️</li><li>After eating, <b>clear the table</b> and wash the dishes. 🧽</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-expressions"><strong>⚠️ lend a hand / pitch in / give a hand</strong><div class="diff"><b>lend a hand</b> — помочь кому-то (временно)<br><b>pitch in</b> — внести вклад в общее дело (вместе с другими)<br><b>give a hand</b> — помочь (синоним lend a hand)<div class="examples-box-expressions"><span class="examples-label-expressions">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-expressions"><li>Can you <b>lend me a hand</b> with this heavy box? 📦</li><li>Everyone needs to <b>pitch in</b> to clean the house. 🏠</li><li>I'll <b>give you a hand</b> with the dishes. 🍽️</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-expressions"><strong>⚠️ do the chores vs do the housework</strong><div class="diff"><b>do the chores</b> — выполнять конкретные обязанности по дому (мытьё посуды, уборка)<br><b>do the housework</b> — делать домашнюю работу в целом (все дела)<div class="examples-box-expressions"><span class="examples-label-expressions">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-expressions"><li>I hate <b>doing the chores</b> on weekends. 😒</li><li>She <b>does all the housework</b> while he works. 🏡</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-expressions"><strong>⚠️ pull one's weight vs do one's share</strong><div class="diff"><b>pull one's weight</b> — делать свою долю работы (не лениться)<br><b>do one's share</b> — выполнять свою часть (обязанностей)<div class="examples-box-expressions"><span class="examples-label-expressions">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-expressions"><li>Everyone must <b>pull their weight</b> in this team. 💪</li><li>I always <b>do my share</b> of the cleaning. 🧹</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-expressions"><strong>⚠️ tidy up one's act vs clean up one's act</strong><div class="diff"><b>tidy up one's act</b> — взяться за ум, начать делать всё правильно<br><b>clean up one's act</b> — исправиться, перестать плохо себя вести<div class="examples-box-expressions"><span class="examples-label-expressions">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-expressions"><li>You need to <b>tidy up your act</b> or you'll fail! 📚</li><li>He needs to <b>clean up his act</b> and stop being lazy. 😤</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="practice-box-expressions"><h4>🎯 Practice Text: Family Responsibilities 👨‍‍👧👦</h4><div class="practice-text-expressions">In our family, everyone needs to <span class="mask-wrapper-expressions" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-expressions">pull their weight</span><span class="mask-overlay-expressions"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-expressions">📌 делать свою долю работы</span></span>. My mom <span class="mask-wrapper-expressions" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-expressions">does most of the housework</span><span class="mask-overlay-expressions"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-expressions">📌 делает домашнюю работу</span></span>, but we all help too. Before dinner, I <span class="mask-wrapper-expressions" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-expressions">set the table</span><span class="mask-overlay-expressions"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-expressions">📌 накрыть на стол</span></span> with plates and cutlery. After eating, my brother <span class="mask-wrapper-expressions" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-expressions">clears the table</span><span class="mask-overlay-expressions"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-expressions">📌 убирает со стола</span></span> and puts the dishes in the dishwasher. When my dad needs help with something heavy, I always <span class="mask-wrapper-expressions" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-expressions">lend him a hand</span><span class="mask-overlay-expressions"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-expressions">📌 помочь</span></span>. On weekends, we all <span class="mask-wrapper-expressions" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-expressions">pitch in</span><span class="mask-overlay-expressions"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-expressions">📌 внести вклад</span></span> to clean the whole house — everyone <span class="mask-wrapper-expressions" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-expressions">does their share</span><span class="mask-overlay-expressions"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-expressions">📌 выполняет свою часть</span></span>. I hate <span class="mask-wrapper-expressions" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-expressions">doing the chores</span><span class="mask-overlay-expressions"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-expressions">📌 выполнять обязанности</span></span>, but it's necessary. My sister is very lazy, so mom told her she needs to <span class="mask-wrapper-expressions" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-expressions">tidy up her act</span><span class="mask-overlay-expressions"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-expressions">📌 взяться за ум</span></span> or she'll lose her allowance.
