class="dr-title-adjectives">Adjectives</h2><button class="dr-btn-adjectives learn" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">📘 Learn Constructions</button><div class="dr-panel-adjectives"><div class="cf-adjectives"><strong>⚠️ UN- группа (самый частый префикс)</strong><div class="diff"><b>tidy / untidy</b> — опрятный / неаккуратный<br><b>clean / unclean</b> — чистый / грязный<br><b>safe / unsafe</b> — безопасный / опасный<br><b>washed / unwashed</b> — мытый / немытый<br><b>swept / unswept</b> — подметённый / неподметённый<div class="examples-box-adjectives"><span class="examples-label-adjectives">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-adjectives"><li>The room is very <b>untidy</b> — clothes everywhere! 🧺</li><li>These dishes are still <b>unclean</b>. 🍽️</li><li>This chemical is <b>unsafe</b> for children. ⚠️</li><li>The <b>unwashed</b> clothes are in the basket. 👕</li><li>The floor is <b>unswept</b> — there's dust everywhere. 🧹</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-adjectives"><strong>⚠️ IN- группа (перед гласными и n)</strong><div class="diff"><b>complete / incomplete</b> — завершённый / незавершённый<br><b>sufficient / insufficient</b> — достаточный / недостаточный<div class="examples-box-adjectives"><span class="examples-label-adjectives">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-adjectives"><li>The cleaning is <b>incomplete</b> — you missed the corners! 🧹</li><li>We have <b>insufficient</b> cleaning supplies. 🧽</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-adjectives"><strong>⚠️ DIS- группа</strong><div class="diff"><b>orderly / disorderly</b> — организованный / хаотичный<div class="examples-box-adjectives"><span class="examples-label-adjectives">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-adjectives"><li>Her closet is very <b>orderly</b> — everything in its place. 📁</li><li>The crowd was <b>disorderly</b> and noisy. 🎪</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-adjectives"><strong>⚠️ IM- группа (только перед M и P)</strong><div class="diff"><b>perfect / imperfect</b> — идеальный / неидеальный<div class="examples-box-adjectives"><span class="examples-label-adjectives">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-adjectives"><li>The house is <b>imperfect</b>, but it's home. 🏠</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-adjectives"><strong>⚠️ MIS- группа</strong><div class="diff"><b>placed / misplaced</b> — положенный (правильно) / неправильно положенный<div class="examples-box-adjectives"><span class="examples-label-adjectives">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-adjectives"><li>I can't find my keys — they're <b>misplaced</b> again! 🔑</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-adjectives"><strong>⚠️ Особый случай: unswept vs dusty</strong><div class="diff"><b>unswept</b> — неподметённый (не убран веником)<br><b>dusty</b> — пыльный (покрыт пылью)<div class="examples-box-adjectives"><span class="examples-label-adjectives">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-adjectives"><li>The floor is <b>unswept</b> — I need to sweep it. 🧹</li><li>The shelves are <b>dusty</b> — I need to wipe them. 🕸️</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="practice-box-adjectives"><h4>🎯 Practice Text: House Inspection 🏠</h4><div class="practice-text-adjectives">My mom is very strict about cleanliness. Yesterday she inspected my room and said it was <span class="mask-wrapper-adjectives" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-adjectives">untidy</span><span class="mask-overlay-adjectives"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-adjectives">📌 неаккуратный</span></span> — clothes everywhere! She also noticed that the dishes were still <span class="mask-wrapper-adjectives" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-adjectives">unclean</span><span class="mask-overlay-adjectives"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-adjectives">📌 грязный</span></span> in the sink. "This is <span class="mask-wrapper-adjectives" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-adjectives">unsafe</span><span class="mask-overlay-adjectives"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-adjectives">📌 опасный</span></span>!" she said, pointing at the cleaning chemicals I left on the floor. Then she saw the <span class="mask-wrapper-adjectives" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-adjectives">unwashed</span><span class="mask-overlay-adjectives"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-adjectives">📌 немытый</span></span> clothes in my basket and the <span class="mask-wrapper-adjectives" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-adjectives">unswept</span><span class="mask-overlay-adjectives"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-adjectives">📌 неподметённый</span></span> floor with dust everywhere. "The shelves are <span class="mask-wrapper-adjectives" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-adjectives">dusty</span><span class="mask-overlay-adjectives"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-adjectives">📌 пыльный</span></span> too!" she added. My cleaning was clearly <span class="mask-wrapper-adjectives" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-adjectives">incomplete</span><span class="mask-overlay-adjectives"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-adjectives">📌 незавершённый</span></span>. She said we had <span class="mask-wrapper-adjectives" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-adjectives">insufficient</span><span class="mask-overlay-adjectives"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-adjectives">📌 недостаточный</span></span> cleaning supplies, so we needed to buy more. My closet was <span class="mask-wrapper-adjectives" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-adjectives">disorderly</span><span class="mask-overlay-adjectives"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-adjectives">📌 хаотичный</span></span> — nothing in its place. "Nothing is <span class="mask-wrapper-adjectives" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-adjectives">perfect</span><span class="mask-overlay-adjectives"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-adjectives">📌 идеальный</span></span>," she sighed, "but this is <span class="mask-wrapper-adjectives" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-adjectives">imperfect</span><span class="mask-overlay-adjectives"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-adjectives">📌 неидеальный</span></span>!" I couldn't find my keys — they were <span class="mask-wrapper-adjectives" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-adjectives">misplaced</span><span class="mask-overlay-adjectives"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-adjectives">📌 неправильно положенный</span></span> again! I need to be more <span class="mask-wrapper-adjectives" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-adjectives">tidy</span><span class="mask-overlay-adjectives"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-adjectives">📌 опрятный</span></span> and <span class="mask-wrapper-adjectives" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-adjectives">orderly</span><span class="mask-overlay-adjectives"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-adjectives">📌 организованный</span></span>!</div></div></div><button class="dr-btn-adjectives test" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">✏️ Test: Adjectives</button><div class="dr-panel-adjectives"><h3 class="qz-title-adjectives">Test: Adjectives</h3><p class="qz-sub-adjectives">Choose the correct option:</p><div class="qz-card-adjectives"><div class="qz-num-adjectives">1</div><div class="qz-sent-adjectives">Your room is very ___ — clothes everywhere!</div><input type="radio" name="a_q1" id="a_q1a" class="qz-r-adjectives wrng"><label for="a_q1a" class="qz-opt-adjectives">A) tidy</label><input type="radio" name="a_q1" id="a_q1b" class="qz-r-adjectives corr"><label for="a_q1b" class="qz-opt-adjectives">B) untidy</label><input type="radio" name="a_q1" id="a_q1c" class="qz-r-adjectives wrng"><label for="a_q1c" class="qz-opt-adjectives">C) intidy</label><div class="qz-fb-adjectives ok">✅ Correct! <b>Untidy</b> = неаккуратный (UN-).</div><div class="qz-fb-adjectives no">❌ A) Tidy = опрятный. C) <b>Intidy</b> — неправильно! Правильный префикс UN-.</div></div><div class="qz-card-adjectives"><div class="qz-num-adjectives">2</div><div class="qz-sent-adjectives">These dishes are still ___ — you need to wash them!</div><input type="radio" name="a_q2" id="a_q2a" class="qz-r-adjectives wrng"><label for="a_q2a" class="qz-opt-adjectives">A) clean</label><input type="radio" name="a_q2" id="a_q2b" class="qz-r-adjectives corr"><label for="a_q2b" class="qz-opt-adjectives">B) unclean</label><input type="radio" name="a_q2" id="a_q2c" class="qz-r-adjectives wrng"><label for="a_q2c" class="qz-opt-adjectives">C) inclean</label><div class="qz-fb-adjectives ok">✅ Correct! <b>Unclean</b> = грязный (UN-).</div><div class="qz-fb-adjectives no">❌ A) Clean = чистый. C) <b>Inclean</b> — неправильно! Правильный префикс UN-.</div></div><div class="qz-card-adjectives"><div class="qz-num-adjectives">3</div><div class="qz-sent-adjectives">This chemical is ___ for children — keep it away!</div><input type="radio" name="a_q3" id="a_q3a" class="qz-r-adjectives wrng"><label for="a_q3a" class="qz-opt-adjectives">A) safe</label><input type="radio" name="a_q3" id="a_q3b" class="qz-r-adjectives corr"><label for="a_q3b" class="qz-opt-adjectives">B) unsafe</label><input type="radio" name="a_q3" id="a_q3c" class="qz-r-adjectives wrng"><label for="a_q3c" class="qz-opt-adjectives">C) insafe</label><div class="qz-fb-adjectives ok">✅ Correct! <b>Unsafe</b> = опасный (UN-).</div><div class="qz-fb-adjectives no">❌ A) Safe = безопасный. C) <b>Insafe</b> — неправильно! Правильный префикс UN-.</div></div><div class="qz-card-adjectives"><div class="qz-num-adjectives">4</div><div class="qz-sent-adjectives">The cleaning is ___ — you missed the corners!</div><input type="radio" name="a_q4" id="a_q4a" class="qz-r-adjectives wrng"><label for="a_q4a" class="qz-opt-adjectives">A) complete</label><input type="radio" name="a_q4" id="a_q4b" class="qz-r-adjectives corr"><label for="a_q4b" class="qz-opt-adjectives">B) incomplete</label><input type="radio" name="a_q4" id="a_q4c" class="qz-r-adjectives wrng"><label for="a_q4c" class="qz-opt-adjectives">C) uncomplete</label><div class="qz-fb-adjectives ok">✅ Correct! <b>Incomplete</b> = незавершённый (IN-).</div><div class="qz-fb-adjectives no">❌ A) Complete = завершённый. C) <b>Uncomplete</b> — неправильно! Правильный префикс IN-.</div></div><div class="qz-card-adjectives"><div class="qz-num-adjectives">5</div><div class="qz-sent-adjectives">We have ___ cleaning supplies — we need to buy more!</div><input type="radio" name="a_q5" id="a_q5a" class="qz-r-adjectives wrng"><label for="a_q5a" class="qz-opt-adjectives">A) sufficient</label><input type="radio" name="a_q5" id="a_q5b" class="qz-r-adjectives corr"><label for="a_q5b" class="qz-opt-adjectives">B) insufficient</label><input type="radio" name="a_q5" id="a_q5c" class="qz-r-adjectives wrng"><label for="a_q5c" class="qz-opt-adjectives">C) unsufficient</label><div class="qz-fb-adjectives ok">✅ Correct! <b>Insufficient</b> = недостаточный (IN-).</div><div class="qz-fb-adjectives no">❌ A) Sufficient = достаточный. C) <b>Unsufficient</b> — неправильно! Правильный префикс IN-.</div></div><div class="qz-card-adjectives"><div class="qz-num-adjectives">6</div><div class="qz-sent-adjectives">Her closet is very ___ — everything in its place!</div><input type="radio" name="a_q6" id="a_q6a" class="qz-r-adjectives corr"><label for="a_q6a" class="qz-opt-adjectives">A) orderly</label><input type="radio" name="a_q6" id="a_q6b" class="qz-r-adjectives wrng"><label for="a_q6b" class="qz-opt-adjectives">B) disorderly</label><input type="radio" name="a_q6" id="a_q6c" class="qz-r-adjectives wrng"><label for="a_q6c" class="qz-opt-adjectives">C) unorderly</label><div class="qz-fb-adjectives ok">✅ Correct! <b>Orderly</b> = организованный.</div><div class="qz-fb-adjectives no">❌ B) Disorderly = хаотичный. C) <b>Unorderly</b> — неправильно! Правильный префикс DIS-.</div></div><div class="qz-card-adjectives"><div class="qz-num-adjectives">7</div><div class="qz-sent-adjectives">The house is ___, but it's home.</div><input type="radio" name="a_q7" id="a_q7a" class="qz-r-adjectives wrng"><label for="a_q7a" class="qz-opt-adjectives">A) perfect</label><input type="radio" name="a_q7" id="a_q7b" class="qz-r-adjectives corr"><label for="a_q7b" class="qz-opt-adjectives">B) imperfect</label><input type="radio" name="a_q7" id="a_q7c" class="qz-r-adjectives wrng"><label for="a_q7c" class="qz-opt-adjectives">C) unperfect</label><div class="qz-fb-adjectives ok">✅ Correct! <b>Imperfect</b> = неидеальный (IM- перед P).</div><div class="qz-fb-adjectives no">❌ A) Perfect = идеальный. C) <b>Unperfect</b> — неправильно! Правильный префикс IM- (перед P).</div></div><div class="qz-card-adjectives"><div class="qz-num-adjectives">8</div><div class="qz-sent-adjectives">I can't find my keys — they're ___ again!</div><input type="radio" name="a_q8" id="a_q8a" class="qz-r-adjectives wrng"><label for="a_q8a" class="qz-opt-adjectives">A) placed</label><input type="radio" name="a_q8" id="a_q8b" class="qz-r-adjectives corr"><label for="a_q8b" class="qz-opt-adjectives">B) misplaced</label><input type="radio" name="a_q8" id="a_q8c" class="qz-r-adjectives wrng"><label for="a_q8c" class="qz-opt-adjectives">C) unplac`} />
<InlineCode code={`<!DOCTYPE html><html lang="ru"><head><meta charset="UTF-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1.0"><title>Word Formation</title><style>body{font-family:Arial,sans-serif;display:flex;justify-content:center;padding:20px;background:#f5f5f5}.container{background:#fff;border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.1);overflow:hidden;max-width:1000px;width:100%}h2{margin:0;padding:15px 20px;background:#4a90e2;color:#fff;font-size:18px}table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse}thead{background:#4a90e2;color:#fff}th,td{padding:10px 8px;text-align:center;vertical-align:middle}th{font-weight:600;white-space:nowrap}td{border-bottom:1px solid #eee;word-break:break-word}tbody tr:hover{background:#f9f9f9}.word{display:inline-block;padding:2px 4px;color:#333;font-size:13px;line-height:1.4}.noun-suffix{color:#1565c0;font-weight:700}.neg-prefix{color:#d32f2f;font-weight:700}.adv-suffix{color:#388e3c;font-weight:700}.adj-suffix{color:#f57c00;font-weight:700}.dash{color:#999;text-align:center}</style></head><body><div class="container"><h2>📚 Word Formation</h2><table><thead><tr><th>💼 Noun</th><th>📝 Verb</th><th>💎 Adjective</th><th>⚡ Adverb</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><span class="word">tid<span class="noun-suffix">iness</span></span></td><td><span class="word">tidy</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">un</span>tidy</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">un</span>tid<span class="adv-suffix">ily</span></span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">cleanli<span class="noun-suffix">ness</span></span></td><td><span class="word">clean</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">un</span>clean</span></td><td><span class="word">clean<span class="adv-suffix">ly</span></span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">order</span></td><td><span class="word">order</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">dis</span>order<span class="adj-suffix">ly</span></span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">dis</span>order<span class="adv-suffix">ly</span></span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">safe<span class="noun-suffix">ty</span></span></td><td><span class="word">safeguard</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">un</span>safe</span></td><td><span class="word">safe<span class="adv-suffix">ly</span></span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">perfect<span class="noun-suffix">ion</span></span></td><td><span class="word">perfect</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">im</span>perfect</span></td><td><span class="word">perfect<span class="adv-suffix">ly</span></span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">sufficien<span class="noun-suffix">cy</span></span></td><td><span class="word">suffice</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">in</span>sufficien<span class="adj-suffix">t</span></span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">in</span>sufficien<span class="adv-suffix">tly</span></span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">adequa<span class="noun-suffix">cy</span></span></td><td><span class="word">adequate</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">in</span>adequate</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">in</span>adequat<span class="adv-suffix">ely</span></span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">respect</span></td><td><span class="word">respect</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">dis</span>respect<span class="adj-suffix">ful</span></span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">dis</span>respect<span class="adv-suffix">fully</span></span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">fair<span class="noun-suffix">ness</span></span></td><td><span class="word">fair</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">un</span>fair</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">un</span>fair<span class="adv-suffix">ly</span></span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">correct<span class="noun-suffix">ion</span></span></td><td><span class="word">correct</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">in</span>correct</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">in</span>correct<span class="adv-suffix">ly</span></span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">reasonable<span class="noun-suffix">ness</span></span></td><td><span class="word">reason</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">un</span>reasonable</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">un</span>reason<span class="adv-suffix">ably</span></span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">expect<span class="noun-suffix">ation</span></span></td><td><span class="word">expect</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">un</span>expect<span class="adj-suffix">ed</span></span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">un</span>expect<span class="adj-suffix">ed</span><span class="adv-suffix">ly</span></span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">convenien<span class="noun-suffix">ce</span></span></td><td><span class="word">convene</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">in</span>convenient</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">in</span>convenient<span class="adv-suffix">ly</span></span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">mis</span>place<span class="noun-suffix">ment</span></span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">mis</span>place</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">mis</span>plac<span class="adj-suffix">ed</span></span></td><td class="dash">—</td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">sweep<span class="noun-suffix">ing</span></span></td><td><span class="word">sweep</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">un</span>swept</span></td><td class="dash">—</td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">wash<span class="noun-suffix">ing</span></span></td><td><span class="word">wash</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">un</span>wash<span class="adj-suffix">ed</span></span></td><td class="dash">—</td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">polish</span></td><td><span class="word">polish</span></td><td><span class="word">polish<span class="adj-suffix">ed</span></span></td><td class="dash">—</td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">fold<span class="noun-suffix">ing</span></span></td><td><span class="word">fold</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">un</span>fold<span class="adj-suffix">ed</span></span></td><td class="dash">—</td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">sort<span class="noun-suffix">ing</span></span></td><td><span class="word">sort</span></td><td><span class="word">sort<span class="adj-suffix">ed</span></span></td><td class="dash">—</td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">scrub<span class="noun-suffix">bing</span></span></td><td><span class="word">scrub</span></td><td><span class="word">scrub<span class="adj-suffix">bed</span></span></td><td class="dash">—</td></tr></tbody></table></div></body></html>`} />
<InlineCode code={`<div style="max-width:1100px;margin:0 auto;font-family:'Segoe UI',system-ui,sans-serif;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#667eea 0%,#764ba2 100%);padding:16px;border-radius:16px"><style>*{box-sizing:border-box}.tabs{display:flex;gap:6px;margin-bottom:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;justify-content:center}.tab-radio{display:none}.tab-label{padding:10px 16px;background:#fff;color:#667eea;border-radius:20px;cursor:pointer;font-weight:700;font-size:14px;transition:all 0.3s;box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.1);white-space:nowrap}.tab-label:hover{transform:translateY(-2px)}.tab-content{display:none;animation:fadeSlide 0.3s ease}.tab-radio:checked+.tab-label{background:linear-gradient(135deg,#11998e,#38ef7d);color:#fff;box-shadow:0 4px 15px rgba(17,153,142,0.4)}#t1:checked~.c1,#t2:checked~.c2,#t3:checked~.c3,#t4:checked~.c4,#t5:checked~.c5,#t6:checked~.c6,#t7:checked~.c7,#t8:checked~.c8,#t9:checked~.c9,#t10:checked~.c10{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(380px,1fr));gap:14px}@keyframes fadeSlide{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(10px)}to{opacity:1;transform:translateY(0)}}.flip-card{perspective:1000px;height:320px;cursor:pointer;position:relative}.flip-input{display:none}.flip-inner{position:relative;width:100%;height:100%;transition:transform 0.6s cubic-bezier(0.4,0,0.2,1);transform-style:preserve-3d}.flip-card input:checked+.flip-inner{transform:rotateY(180deg)}.flip-front,.flip-back{position:absolute;width:100%;height:100%;backface-visibility:hidden;border-radius:16px;padding:16px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;justify-content:center;align-items:flex-start;text-align:left;box-shadow:0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,0.25)}.flip-front{background:#fff}.flip-back{background:linear-gradient(135deg,#e8f4f8 0%,#d1ecf1 100%);color:#1a3a4a;transform:rotateY(180deg)}.q-num{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;width:38px;height:38px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#667eea,#764ba2);color:#fff;border-radius:50%;font-weight:800;font-size:18px;margin-bottom:12px;flex-shrink:0}.q-text{font-weight:700;line-height:1.45;margin:0;color:#2c3e50}.flip-front .q-text{font-size:24px}.flip-back .q-text{color:#1a3a4a;font-size:22px}.flip-hint{margin-top:auto;font-size:12px;opacity:0.5;font-weight:600;letter-spacing:0.5px;text-transform:uppercase}.mark{background:#fff3cd;color:#856404;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:5px;font-weight:700}.flip-back .mark{background:#fff;color:#0d7377;border:2px solid #38ef7d}</style><h1 style="color:#fff;text-align:center;margin:0 0 14px;font-size:26px;text-shadow:0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.2);font-weight:800;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis">🧹 Household Chores Interview Questions</h1><input type="radio" name="tabs" id="t1" class="tab-radio" checked><input type="radio" name="tabs" id="t2" class="tab-radio"><input type="radio" name="tabs" id="t3" class="tab-radio"><input type="radio" name="tabs" id="t4" class="tab-radio"><input type="radio" name="tabs" id="t5" class="tab-radio"><input type="radio" name="tabs" id="t6" class="tab-radio"><input type="radio" name="tabs" id="t7" class="tab-radio"><input type="radio" name="tabs" id="t8" class="tab-radio"><input type="radio" name="tabs" id="t9" class="tab-radio"><input type="radio" name="tabs" id="t10" class="tab-radio"><div class="tabs"><label for="t1" class="tab-label">🧹 Daily Chores</label><label for="t2" class="tab-label">👕 Laundry</label><label for="t3" class="tab-label">🍽️ Kitchen</label><label for="t4" class="tab-label">🛁 Bathroom</label><label for="t5" class="tab-label">🧽 Deep Clean</label><label for="t6" class="tab-label">🗂️ Organization</label><label for="t7" class="tab-label">👨‍👩‍👧 Family</label><label for="t8" class="tab-label">🧼 Products</label><label for="t9" class="tab-label">⏰ Time Mgmt</label><label for="t10" class="tab-label">🌟 Motivation</label></div><div class="tab-content c1"><label class="flip-card"><input type="checkbox" class="flip-input"><div class="flip-inner"><div class="flip-front"><span class="q-num">1</span><p class="q-text">🧹 What <mark>chores</mark> do you usually <mark>do</mark> every day at home?</p><span class="flip-hint">↻ Tap to flip</span></div><div class="flip-back"><p class="q-text">I usually <mark>make the bed</mark> and <mark>tidy up</mark> my room every morning. I also <mark>help</mark> my parents <mark>clear the table</mark> after dinner.</p></div></div></label><label class="flip-card"><input type="checkbox" class="flip-input"><div class="flip-inner"><div class="flip-front"><span class="q-num">2</span><p class="q-text">🧹 How do you <mark>keep things tidy</mark> in your room?</p><span class="flip-hint">↻ Tap to flip</span></div><div class="flip-back"><p class="q-text">I try to <mark>put things away</mark> right after using them. This <mark>habit</mark> helps me <mark>maintain cleanliness</mark> without spending too much time.</p></div></div></label><label class="flip-card"><input type="checkbox" class="flip-input"><div class="flip-inner"><div class="flip-front"><span class="q-num">3</span><p class="q-text">🧹 Do you prefer to <mark>clean up</mark> in the morning or evening? Why?</p><span class="flip-hint">↻ Tap to flip</span></div><div class="flip-back"><p class="q-text">I prefer to <mark>tidy up</mark> in the evening because it helps me <mark>start</mark> the next day with a <mark>clean slate</mark>. It also helps me relax before bed.</p></div></div></label><label class="flip-card"><input type="checkbox" class="flip-input"><div class="flip-inner"><div class="flip-front"><span class="q-num">4</span><p class="q-text">🧹 What do you do when you see a <mark>stain</mark> on your clothes?</p><span class="flip-hint">↻ Tap to flip</span></div><div class="flip-back"><p class="q-text">I usually <mark>try</mark> to <mark>scrub</mark> it gently with <mark>detergent</mark>.
